**[00:30:16.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000207)  
Noong nakaraang taon, katuwang natin ang mga kasamahan sa Senado at sa pangunguna ni then Finance Chair

**[00:30:23.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000208)  
at ngayon ay Senate President Sherwin Gatchalian.

**[00:30:25.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000209)  
Sinuporta natin ang paglalaan ng karagdagang pondo para sa AFP Modernization.

**[00:30:31.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000210)  
Sa ilalim ng inyong kasalukoy budget para sa 2026, umabot sa 90 billion ang pinagsamang alokasyon

**[00:30:38.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000211)  
para sa revised AFP Modernization Program, 40 billion sa program appropriations

**[00:30:43.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000212)  
at 50 billion sa unprogrammed appropriations.

**[00:30:46.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000213)  
Nais po natin malaman kung gaano nakalaki sa pondong ito ang aktual na nailabas, na obliga at nagastos

**[00:30:53.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000214)  
at kung ano ang katumbas.

**[00:30:55.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000215)  
At itong progreso sa ating mga proyekto na pangmodernisasyon.

**[00:31:00.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000216)  
Alin sa mga inaasahang kagamitan at sistema ang nahihatid na at nagagamit ng ating masundalo?

**[00:31:06.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000217)  
Anong mga hadlang sa pagpapatupad?

**[00:31:09.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000218)  
At alin ang mga pangangailangan ang dapat nating unahin sa pagbabalangkas ng budget para sa fiscal year 2027?

**[00:31:17.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000219)  
As Chair of the Senate Committee on Finance, and as I have done so for many years,

**[00:31:21.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000220)  
I reaffirm our commitment to sustaining the AFP's modernization

**[00:31:23.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000221)  
with our fiscal constraints.

**[00:31:27.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000222)  
Kapag hinahamak ang ating bandila at kinigipit ang ating mga kababayan sa sarili nating mga karagatan,

**[00:31:35.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000223)  
dapat malinaw ang ating prinsipyo at matatag ang ating tugon.

**[00:31:40.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000224)  
Sa gitna ng maraming mga hamon at kinakaharap ng ating bansa,

**[00:31:43.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000225)  
kailangan buo ang ating loob at nagkakaisang ating pagtindig na mapagtatanggol ng pambansang interes.

**[00:31:52.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000226)  
Ito ang diwa ng pambansang interes.

**[00:31:53.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000228)  
Ito ang pangakong ating binibigas sa tuwing inaawit ang lupang hinirang,

**[00:31:58.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000229)  
na sa harap ng sino mang manlulupig kailanman ay hindi tayo pasisiil.

**[00:32:02.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000230)  
I remain steadfast in my belief that defending our nation's honor and sovereignty

**[00:32:07.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000231)  
through the attainment of a credible defense posture must transcend partisan loyalties,

**[00:32:13.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000232)  
political considerations, and personal interests.

**[00:32:16.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000233)  
The defense of our nation must always rise above politics

**[00:32:20.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000234)  
and remain firmly anchored in our shared commitment

**[00:32:23.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000235)  
to national security, sovereignty, and the Filipino people.

**[00:32:27.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000236)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:32:28.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000237)

**[00:32:33.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000238)

**[00:32:35.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000239)  
So I'd like to ask my colleagues if they have any opening statement.

**[00:32:39.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000240)  
Let me start with our Senate President.

**[00:32:42.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000241)  
Magandang hapon, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:32:44.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000242)  
Magandang hapon, Secretary Teodoro, sa lahat po ng ating kasama sa Armed Forces of the Philippines.

**[00:32:51.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000243)  
Talaga pong mabilis pong nagbabalik.

**[00:32:53.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000244)  
Pabago ang geopolitical situation dito sa ating region at sa buong mundo na rin.

**[00:32:59.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000245)  
Nakikita po natin yung iba't ibang mga conflict na nangyari sa Middle East,

**[00:33:05.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000246)  
sa Eastern Europe, at sa iba pang parte ng buong mundo.

**[00:33:11.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000247)  
At sa ganitong punto, importante po na maging handa tayo

**[00:33:15.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000248)  
at patuloy po natin minomodernize at pinapalakas po ang ating Armed Forces.

**[00:33:20.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000249)  
Kasama na rin po dito yung pagdating po sa cyber warfare.

**[00:33:23.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000250)  
Marami rin po akong pabalitaan at napapanood po sa balita

**[00:33:28.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000251)  
na ang cyber warfare ay isa po sa mga bagay na nagiging realidad na sa mga ganito pong sitwasyon.

**[00:33:40.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000252)  
Alam ko, komplikado po itong cyberspace pero ganun pa man,

**[00:33:44.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000253)  
importante na nakakabili [unclear] po tayo ng mga

**[00:33:53.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000254)  
kagamitan na makakatulong po tayo sa pagprepara sa mga ganitong conflicts at warfare.

**[00:34:03.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000255)  
At sa kabilang punto, ang Armed Forces of the Philippines ay hindi lang tag-defend ng ating bansa

**[00:34:13.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000256)  
kung hindi rin rin meresponder ito sa mga kalamidad na nangyayari po sa ating bansa.

**[00:34:18.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000257)  
At isa rin po sa mga bagay na dapat po natin tignan,

**[00:34:23.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000258)  
multi-role capability ng ating armed forces, lalo na pag tumatama sa ating malaking bagyo

**[00:34:31.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000259)  
o mayroong mga malalaking kalamidad na nararanasan natin.

**[00:34:36.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000260)  
So kami po ay sumusuporta sa pagbibigay ng dagdag kapasidad ng ating armed forces

**[00:34:44.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000261)  
at importante rin po na tuloy-tuloy yung supporta, sustainable yung pagsusuporta natin

**[00:34:49.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000262)  
hanggat umabot tayo sa kakayahan na gusto natin makita.

**[00:34:53.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000263)  
So maraming salamat, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:34:55.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000264)  
Thank you, our Senate President. Maraming salamat din sa supporta mo nung isang taon sa ating armed forces.

**[00:35:02.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000265)  
The biggest ever, probably, budget for the AFP modernization.

**[00:35:06.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000266)  
Our colleague, Senator Panfilo Lacson.

**[00:35:09.960] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000267)  
None from this representation. I'll just listen to the presentation and seek clarification if I need to. Thank you.

**[00:35:16.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000268)  
Thank you, Senator Lacson, Senator Tulfo.

**[00:35:19.000] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000269)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair. Magandang araw po sa ating lahat.

**[00:35:22.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000270)  
Today, as we deliberate the proposed 2027 budget of the Department of National Defense,

**[00:35:29.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000271)  
let us be guided by our commitment to protect our sovereignty, uphold our territorial integrity,

**[00:35:38.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000272)  
and ensure the safety and security of every Filipinos.

**[00:35:42.940] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000273)  
But how can we do that if the budget of the National Defense is cut down,

**[00:35:48.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000274)  
if I'm not mistaken, about 43.01%?

**[00:35:52.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000275)  
Bapano po natin mapoprotektahan ang ating soberenya,

**[00:35:58.780] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000276)  
pagtatanggol po ang ating teritoryo,

**[00:36:01.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000277)  
at masiguro ang siguridad at kapakanan ng bawat Pilipino kung binawasan po ng budget?

**[00:36:09.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000278)  
Ang budget na ito ay hindi lamang tungkol sa numbers and allocations.

**[00:36:12.080] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000279)  
It represents our investment in stronger, more capable, and more modern armed forces of the Philippines.

**[00:36:19.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000280)  
Ang bawat pisong inilalaan sa pambansang depensa ay dapat,

**[00:36:22.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000281)  
magsilbi sa kapakanan ng sambayan ng Pilipino.

**[00:36:25.260] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000282)  
Every allocation must translate into tangible improvements in our country's defense, posture,

**[00:36:31.780] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000283)  
operational readiness, and ability to respond to both traditional emerging security challenges.

**[00:36:39.000] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000284)  
Nabawasan nga po ang external threat.

**[00:36:42.220] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000285)  
Ang ating internal threat, tumaas naman po ang external threat, meaning kalaban galing sa labas.

**[00:36:49.760] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000286)  
How do we address that kung mas maliit po na budget?

**[00:36:53.060] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000287)  
We also recognize that national defense goes beyond preparedness.

**[00:36:58.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000288)  
It encompasses our commitment to our soldiers and their families,

**[00:37:01.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000289)  
our support for communities affected by conflict and disasters,

**[00:37:05.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000290)  
and our contribution to a peaceful, secure, and resilient Philippines.

**[00:37:09.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000291)  
We look forward to a constructive discussion of DND's 2027 proposed budget

**[00:37:14.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000292)  
to ensure that every peso strategically allocated, responsibly spent,

**[00:37:19.700] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000293)  
and aligned with our national defense priorities.

**[00:37:22.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-000294)  
Maraming salamat po, Gen. Chairman.

**[00:37:26.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000295)  
Thank you, Senator Tulfo.

**[00:37:29.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000296)  
Before we ask the Department of National Defense and the AFP for the presentation,

**[00:37:34.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000297)  
I seek the indulgence of our colleagues to allow the department and the touch agency

**[00:37:38.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000298)  
to conclude their presentation before asking questions.

**[00:37:42.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000299)  
Each center will be allowed later with about 10 minutes for questions.

**[00:37:46.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000300)  
In keeping tradition, of course, we will give priority to the officials of the Senate,

**[00:37:50.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000301)  
followed by the centers.

**[00:37:52.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000302)  
In the order of the arrival.

**[00:37:54.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000303)  
So at this point, at this juncture, I would like now to call to our Secretary of National Defense,

**[00:38:00.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000304)  
Gilbert Teodoro, Jr., to deliver his opening statement and the presentation of the department

**[00:38:05.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000305)  
and its attached agencies thereafter.

**[00:38:07.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000306)  
Thank you.

**[00:38:08.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000307)  
Secretary Teodoro, you now have the floor.

**[00:38:10.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000308)  
Thank you very much, Mr. Chairperson, and to our Senate President and our sponsor last year,

**[00:38:16.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000309)  
to our Senator Lacson, Vice Chairman, and Senator Tulfo, Vice Chairman, likewise.

**[00:38:22.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000310)  
It is indeed an honor to present our budget request once again to this honorable chamber.

**[00:38:31.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000311)  
And might I just give a short preface and then if with the indulgence of the senators,

**[00:38:42.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000312)  
go straight to page 15 of the presentation, which is the highlights of the proposed budget per NET.

**[00:38:52.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000313)  
Firstly, we, the Armed Forces of the Philippines, at the forefront together with the Department of National Defense in cooperation with other agencies, have been at the forefront of external defense operations in order to deter further aggression in the West Philippine Sea and other areas now not traditionally encompassed.

**[00:39:22.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000314)  
Secondly, there still is robust presence to preserve the gains of internal security

**[00:39:32.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000315)  
in areas which have been cleared of the CPP, NPA, and DF threat.

**[00:39:39.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000316)  
Nonetheless, we estimate that robust presence of the Armed Forces of the Philippines,

**[00:39:47.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000317)  
considering that the coverage of the Armed Forces of the Philippines,

**[00:39:52.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000318)  
not only for operational purposes, but also for administrative and logistical purposes,

**[00:39:59.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000319)  
is not as robust within the 7,600 islands as we want.

**[00:40:06.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000320)  
For this reason, the Philippine Army has been tasked to scope its areas of operations

**[00:40:14.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000321)  
and also to determine for logistical and staging purposes

**[00:40:19.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000322)  
and support purposes for our military.

**[00:40:22.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000323)  
For our strategic basing, which we have proposed,

**[00:40:26.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000324)  
it is necessary to establish new presence, whether these be brigades or divisions.

**[00:40:33.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000325)  
Secondly, these areas will serve as nodes for our critical infrastructure,

**[00:40:39.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000326)  
which has to be, as we determined, stand-alone.

**[00:40:44.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000327)  
You mentioned earlier our cyber systems.

**[00:40:50.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000328)  
We do have capabilities.

**[00:40:52.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000329)  
However, these rely on the, shall we say,

**[00:41:02.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000330)  
sterile nature of critical basic infrastructure,

**[00:41:07.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000331)  
which we have had to remediate and deal with.

**[00:41:12.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000332)  
As you know, in terms of IT, you start with IT and then you progress to cyber

**[00:41:19.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000333)  
because your IT foundation is...

**[00:41:22.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000334)  
the basis of your resilience and stability.

**[00:41:26.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000335)  
And one compromise in any area can lead to systemic compromises in others.

**[00:41:33.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000336)  
This we had to continue to remediate.

**[00:41:36.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000337)  
And as we construct our new infrastructure,

**[00:41:39.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000338)  
we will incorporate the resilience necessary in these areas.

**[00:41:48.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000339)  
For our Navy,

**[00:41:52.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000340)  
the major thrust once again is not only for the new platforms

**[00:41:57.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000341)  
to complement with the Coast Guard our presence in the 360-degree area,

**[00:42:04.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000342)  
but we have a big gap which we are starting to fill hopefully by the end of this year

**[00:42:14.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000343)  
in our dockyards and our berthing facilities.

**[00:42:19.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000344)  
Our naval bases,

**[00:42:22.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000345)  
our design,

**[00:42:24.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000346)  
except for a few,

**[00:42:28.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000347)  
very few,

**[00:42:30.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000348)  
are not deep nor big enough

**[00:42:32.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000349)  
to support the type of operations that we will need.

**[00:42:36.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000350)  
Thirdly, our Philippine Air Force continues.

**[00:42:40.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000351)  
It's the sustainment of the systems already ordered

**[00:42:49.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000352)  
and the upgrade of those systems.

**[00:42:52.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000358)  
Because we have to upgrade these systems while we try to segue,

**[00:43:02.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000359)  
or to not segue,

**[00:43:03.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000360)  
to complement this with platforms that we can afford in the meantime,

**[00:43:12.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000361)  
which provide a sufficient deterrence for us.

**[00:43:19.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000362)  
The Philippine Marine Corps,

**[00:43:20.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000363)  
We have a new commandant here, Brigadier General Apostol, who was tasked to scope the areas which need marine operations,

**[00:43:34.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000364)  
knowing very well that the Philippine Marine Corps is a maneuver corps.

**[00:43:40.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000365)  
It is not a territorial corps.

**[00:43:42.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000366)  
And with three marine brigades and one support brigade, no, four infantry brigades and one support brigade for 7,600 islands,

**[00:43:57.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000367)  
I think just the math alone shows that it is too small.

**[00:44:02.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000368)  
Secondly, the Air Force or the Navy or them must ensure their mobility and must ensure their firepower.

**[00:44:12.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000369)  
Because a fixed marine unit is not a marine unit.

**[00:44:18.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000370)  
Its essence is its deployability and mobility.

**[00:44:24.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000371)  
So, Brigadier General Apostol, once he is confirmed, Your Honors, hopefully soon,

**[00:44:36.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000372)  
will devote his time to the...

**[00:44:42.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000373)  
...growth plan of the Philippine Marine Corps that includes equipment.

**[00:44:49.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000374)  
We have talked about the internal security dimension,

**[00:44:53.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000375)  
but a lot of operations right now have seen the connection between territorial...

**[00:45:00.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000376)  
defense and internal security. That is in the front of illegal, FIMI and other illegal activities by undesirable state adversaries that have been proliferating, as the Senate President knows, in our space.

**[00:45:24.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000377)  
Which serve to weaken economic resilience, connectivity, and basic infrastructure, and perhaps other forms of malign activity, which we continued in coordination with other, with the law enforcement agencies to disrupt and to discuss.

**[00:45:54.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000378)  
I think that is a major issue right now on internal security of which we devote a lot of cooperative time with the lead agencies, namely the PAO, the NBI.

**[00:46:15.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000379)  
In HADR, of course, it is a constant threat.

**[00:46:20.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000380)  
However, we have refined our deliverables now.

**[00:46:24.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000381)  
To a coordinated response as quick as possible.

**[00:46:29.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000382)  
And we concentrate on that in the OCD so that we do not lose focus on what people need immediately.

**[00:46:41.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000383)  
Because feeling that need alone for those who may be displaced is already a very, very tall task.

**[00:46:50.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000384)  
And to exceed that will eat into the competence.

**[00:46:54.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000385)  
Of the other agencies, which should be doing resilience and other foundational efforts.

**[00:47:06.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000386)  
So we try to balance the need to focus, the need to coordinate with the ability also to coordinate international cooperation.

**[00:47:24.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000387)  
For collective resilience on the veteran side.

**[00:47:31.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000388)  
We continue to innovate.

**[00:47:35.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000389)  
Services to our veterans, which not only will be in terms of payment, but in terms of giving them.

**[00:47:49.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000390)  
Wellness and dignity.

**[00:47:52.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000391)  
So that they can continue.

**[00:47:54.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000392)  
To continue their lives after they have served in a manner which fits their service.

**[00:48:01.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000393)  
The Veterans Memorial Medical Center, as we said, your honors, now has what we call the Valor Clinics to disaggregate basic medical services throughout the country.

**[00:48:23.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000394)  
So that the veterans.

**[00:48:24.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000395)  
Do not need to come to the Veterans Memorial Medical Center.

**[00:48:30.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000396)  
They have a scope that before the spread of the veteran services through the Valor Clinics, only 6% of the veterans were able to avail of medical treatment.

**[00:48:46.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000397)  
And we have reported to the president that the structure itself of the medical center is a cause for high infection rates.

**[00:48:54.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000398)  
Because of its age and because of its design.

**[00:48:58.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000399)  
So it has to be turned down and rebuilt because it is it was made right after liberation, your honors, and it was a dormitory type of hospital, just like the case on Institute before and with today's day and age with the with the strength of viruses that and bacteria.

**[00:49:23.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000400)  
That have.

**[00:49:24.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000401)  
Mutated over time, we cannot tolerate a structure of that kind, therefore, I believe the president has approved phase one where we will be constructing an annex where we transfer the patients while the major reconstruction work is done and there will be a big outpatient service.

**[00:49:54.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000402)  
To.

**[00:49:54.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000403)  
Collocate with the subway exit that is being constructed for the convenience of veterans and the end goal is for it to become a specialized hospital where the outreach clinics cannot handle anymore likewise to be a specialized hospital for cases where the AFP Medical Center cannot handle the case anymore.

**[00:50:24.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000404)  
We have instructed them.

**[00:50:30.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000405)  
To.

**[00:50:33.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000406)  
Free focus their efforts on higher technological munitions and other implements.

**[00:50:46.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000407)  
For so long, the government arsenal has not operated as an arsenal, but as a factory.

**[00:50:54.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000408)  
And it and and we cannot have it continue that way because at the end of the day, higher technological transfer will only happen on a government to government secret basis with our preferred like minded partners and.

**[00:51:19.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000409)  
The government arsenal will be the.

**[00:51:24.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000410)  
Foundation for these venues of joint cooperation on highly potentially.

**[00:51:35.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000411)  
Highly highly confidential platforms and mechanisms and the evolution of the same.

**[00:51:42.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000412)  
So their their concentration on small arms munition ammunition shall shift.

**[00:51:51.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000413)  
To.

**[00:51:53.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000414)  
Indirect.

**[00:51:54.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000415)  
Fired.

**[00:51:54.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000416)  
Weapons cruiser weaponry, which are in demand throughout the world and as a.

**[00:52:01.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000417)  
Forum for government to government cooperation with our like minded and.

**[00:52:09.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000418)  
Shall we say comprehensive strategic partner countries?

**[00:52:15.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000419)  
I gave a brief backdrop.

**[00:52:18.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000420)  
Your honor.

**[00:52:19.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000421)  
Of the.

**[00:52:21.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000422)  
Uh.

**[00:52:22.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000423)  
Uh.

**[00:52:23.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000424)  
Uh.

**[00:52:24.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000425)  
Uh.

**[00:52:25.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000426)  
Uh.

**[00:52:26.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000427)  
Of the agency.

**[00:55:00.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000489)  
Alright. The Office of the Secretary, which is the whole DND, has 0.49 share percent and it was reduced by 31 percent from year 2021,

**[00:55:18.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000490)  
which means to say we have to work more and hire less for less.

**[00:55:25.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000491)  
To date, Your Honor, the DND proper has a fill-up of only 268 warm bodies to include myself.

**[00:55:37.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000492)  
And we have to handle all of these systems. So, it is unsustainable in the long run.

**[00:55:44.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000493)  
And we have already pending in the House, DND rego-organization.

**[00:55:48.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000494)  
Rego-organization bill, which, I mean, for 260 fill-up, 324 approved plantilla personnel.

**[00:55:59.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000495)  
That's way, way too small for the amount of work that we do.

**[00:56:04.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000496)  
Hence, my shiny forehead, which progresses, Your Honor.

**[00:56:10.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000497)  
For the Armed Forces of the Philippines, it has 312 billion, Your Honor, 313.

**[00:56:18.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000498)  
Which is 96 percent of the budget.

**[00:56:24.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000499)  
For the civilian bureaus, Your Honor, it has a 3 percent share in the national expenditure plan.

**[00:56:37.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000500)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:56:41.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000501)  
The breakdown, Your Honor,

**[00:56:44.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000502)  
shows that the DND,

**[00:56:48.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000503)  
the DND proper received a decrease.

**[00:56:50.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000504)  
The government arsenal decreased.

**[00:56:55.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000505)  
NDCP decreased.

**[00:56:59.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000506)  
OCD decreased.

**[00:57:01.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000507)  
And PBAO decreased, Your Honor.

**[00:57:04.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000508)  
I believe these also require some examination.

Kasi

**[00:57:18.960] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000515)

**[00:57:24.400] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000516)  
Yung house version, it's already at 329.

**[00:57:27.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000517)

**[00:57:31.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000519)

**[00:57:37.200] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000521)

**[00:57:39.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000522)  
Opo.

**[00:57:40.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000523)  
Opo.

**[00:57:41.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000524)  
Para mapasalamatan din po namin.

**[00:57:43.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000525)  
Para na ma-update kasi out of date na rin yung 324.

**[00:57:47.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000526)  
Yes, Sir.

**[00:57:47.700] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000527)  
Thank you.

**[00:57:48.960] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-000528)  
Sorry for interjecting.

**[00:57:50.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000529)  
Kasi po, Sir, pag hindi na, di ba, we cannot present the house version hanggat hindi nyo ma-aprubahan kasi ano eh.

**[00:58:00.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000530)  
Pero that's good news, Sir.

**[00:58:02.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000531)  
Thank you very much for the good news.

**[00:58:10.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000532)  
So, Sir, may I proceed to the next slide already?

**[00:58:16.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000533)  
Next slide already.

**[00:58:18.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000534)  
The automatic appropriations, the regular fund, Sir, is 324.63.

**[00:58:26.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000535)  
The automatic appropriations for personnel is 4.1 billion.

**[00:58:33.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000536)  
And the special purpose fund, which is composed of the pensions, is 132 billion.

**[00:58:48.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000537)  
The modernization program is included here with 50 billion.

**[00:58:57.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000538)  
On the next slide.

**[00:59:04.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000539)  
Personal services.

**[00:59:08.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000540)  
We have a slight increase of 179 in line with our...

**[00:59:18.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000541)  
...goal to increase our footprint.

**[00:59:24.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000542)  
On MOU, Sir, which is quite important for us because of the tempo of operations right now,

**[00:59:34.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000543)  
I can say, Your Honors, that our tempo now is beyond 100%.

**[00:59:41.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000544)  
Meaning to say, it's beyond design because of the number of

**[00:59:48.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000548)  
personnel we have to sustain the number of activities we do.

**[00:59:54.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000549)  
That takes into account internal security, HADR...

**[01:00:00.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000550)  
and for joint exercises, retraining.

**[01:00:03.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000551)  
What suffers here is the amount of rest and retraining time that our men and women have.

**[01:00:15.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000552)  
Right now, we do not have enough forces, both on the operational side to have adequate R&R for them.

**[01:00:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000553)  
And secondly, we lack personnel on the support side.

**[01:00:33.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000554)  
The ratio of operating to support personnel is 80-20, more or less, which is unsustainable

**[01:00:41.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000555)  
because logistics, intelligence, planning, we need a robust infrastructure in order to sustain the operations.

**[01:00:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000556)  
So, the MOOE increase is much, much welcome, particularly, Your Honor, with the cost of fuel right now.

**[01:01:08.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000557)  
Last year, it is good that we were able to hedge the cost of fuel and assume that they would rise.

**[01:01:16.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000558)  
So, this year, the increase is relevant.

**[01:01:25.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000559)  
With capital outlay...

**[01:01:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000560)  
With capital outlay, this is where our biggest gap is.

**[01:01:32.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000561)  
We need a lot of infrastructure right now, particularly, Your Honors, on the eastern seaboard,

**[01:01:38.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000562)  
where we do not have naval presence.

**[01:01:42.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000563)  
We do not have substantial infrastructure also in Palawan, not for the naval purposes, but for the army purposes,

**[01:01:55.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000564)  
which will serve...

**[01:01:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000565)  
which will serve as hubs for supporting our strategic bases.

**[01:02:01.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000566)  
Mindoro.

**[01:02:03.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000567)  
Our presence, too, in Mindoro is only one brigade.

**[01:02:09.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000568)  
And with the size of Mindoro, Your Honor, that is not sufficient.

**[01:02:16.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000569)  
In northern Luzon, we have only one division for how many provinces?

**[01:02:21.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000570)  
And we only have one naval force.

**[01:02:26.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000571)  
In that...

**[01:02:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000572)  
In that area, which spans the South China Sea to the Pacific Ocean.

**[01:02:32.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000573)  
So, we really need to refocus our strategic positioning in these areas, Your Honor.

**[01:02:41.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000574)  
And we cannot do that until...

**[01:02:44.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000575)  
sustainably, until the army solidifies its footprint in the Philippines.

**[01:02:50.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000576)  
So, capital outlay, Your Honor, is extremely important.

**[01:02:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000577)  
Also, for building critical infrastructure facilities for security of our platforms and security of our operations.

**[01:03:07.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000578)  
I believe the capital outlay gap has been one of the major gaps that we have found out right now we have to catch up badly with.

**[01:03:23.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000579)  
And it includes also access to our bases.

**[01:03:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000580)  
In terms of the durability of the infrastructure.

**[01:03:33.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000581)  
For example, the roads and bridges that will link our bases.

**[01:03:37.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000582)  
We have found that normal roads and bridges will not be able to support some of the platforms that we have currently because of the weight.

**[01:03:48.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000583)  
So, our mobility is a sacrifice.

**[01:03:53.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000584)  
We cannot really bridge the whole of the Philippines.

**[01:03:56.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000585)  
Yes.

**[01:03:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000586)  
So, the best that we can do is to provide hubs, as many hubs as possible, which will support sustained operations.

**[01:04:11.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000587)  
Secondly, Your Honor, just like any other entity in the world right now, it's not a matter of just in time, but a matter of reserves and stores.

**[01:04:21.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000588)  
And that we are busy building up our reserve and stores.

**[01:04:26.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000589)  
Not only in...

**[01:04:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000590)  
In terms of munitions, but we need it also in terms of petroleum, oil, and lubricants.

**[01:04:35.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000591)  
We have big notional amounts of petroleum ready, but if we do not have physical custody of this, we will be relying on private gas stations to serve our needs, which is a vulnerability of our operating troops.

**[01:04:57.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000593)  
And it poses difficulties for the troops rather than have them serviced in a durable basis, perhaps up to the battalion level.

**[01:05:15.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000594)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:05:21.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000596)  
The breakdown of regular fund versus...

**[01:05:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000597)  
The 2026 and 2027 comparatives, Your Honor.

**[01:05:36.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000598)  
So, we have a slight increase of 6.13% versus last year of 18 billion.

**[01:05:49.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000599)  
But I believe the increase is reflected more in MOOE.

**[01:05:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000600)  
Than any other item.

**[01:06:01.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000601)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:06:10.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000602)  
Modernization, Your Honor.

**[01:06:12.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000603)  
2026 was 90 with the programmed of 40 and unprogrammed of 50.

**[01:06:21.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000604)  
This year, 50-50.

**[01:06:24.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000605)  
Thank you.

**[01:06:26.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000606)  
Pensions.

**[01:06:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000607)  
Pensions have increased by 13.15%.

**[01:06:34.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000608)  
Total.

**[01:06:42.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000609)  
Next slide.

**[01:06:50.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000610)  
For proposals, Your Honor.

**[01:06:53.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000611)  
Your Honor.

**[01:06:55.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000612)  
Our proposals are...

**[01:06:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000613)  
Based on our approximation of threats and costs and sustainability of activities as we plan them based on threats.

**[01:07:10.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000614)  
It's lower by 248 billion compared to the original proposal we made.

**[01:07:21.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000615)  
But this does not include one-time enhancements of...

**[01:07:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000616)  
But this does not include one-time enhancements of equipment and capabilities which will balloon the figure to very very high amounts.

**[01:07:34.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000617)  
So, we just included our basic requirements, Your Honor.

**[01:07:44.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000618)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:07:50.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000619)  
Our breakdown shows as follows, Your Honor.

**[01:07:53.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000620)  
Of course, from the Office of the Secretary...

**[01:07:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000621)  
Secretary, it's less 16% from what was asked.

**[01:08:02.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000622)  
From the Army, it's less by 23%.

**[01:08:07.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000623)  
Air Force, 24%.

**[01:08:10.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000624)  
Navy, 30%.

**[01:08:12.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000625)  
And GHQ, 82%.

**[01:08:15.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000626)  
And why GHQ?

**[01:08:18.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000627)  
GHQ, Your Honor, includes the area commands,

**[01:08:21.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000628)  
which are the principal operating commands.

**[01:08:24.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000629)  
There are five area commands.

**[01:08:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000630)  
And the budget of the area commands come from the GHQ.

**[01:08:32.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000631)  
Our intelligence command, our sustainment command,

**[01:08:36.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000632)  
and importantly, our strategic command,

**[01:08:38.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000633)  
from which our deterrent capabilities will flow,

**[01:08:44.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000634)  
are sourced from the GHQ budget.

**[01:08:48.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000635)  
So this does not mean to say that it is GHQ itself.

**[01:08:53.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000636)  
But the budgets of...

**[01:08:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000637)  
NOLCOM, SOLCOM, WESCOM, BISCOM,

**[01:09:03.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000638)  
EASTMINCOM, and WESTMINCOM.

**[01:09:07.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000639)  
These are the operating budgets that come out of the GHQ budget.

**[01:09:13.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000640)  
So the reduction in GHQ

**[01:09:16.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000641)  
will result in a gap of operations

**[01:09:23.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000642)  
by the area commands,

**[01:09:26.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000643)  
which are the principal operations,

**[01:09:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000644)  
operating commands.

**[01:09:29.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000645)  
And therefore, if that is the case,

**[01:09:32.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000646)  
then we will have to concentrate the budget

**[01:09:35.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000647)  
on the areas where there is a biggest threat possible.

**[01:09:39.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000648)  
And that will open us to gaps in the other areas.

**[01:09:47.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000649)  
In the government arsenal,

**[01:09:50.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000650)  
there's a reduction of 11%.

**[01:09:54.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000651)  
Pag muna.

**[01:09:56.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000652)  
NDCP,

**[01:09:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000653)  
6%.

**[01:09:58.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000654)  
Office of Civil Defense,

**[01:10:00.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000655)  
53%.

**[01:10:01.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000656)  
Philippine Veterans Affairs Office,

**[01:10:05.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000657)  
23%.

**[01:10:06.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000658)  
And Veterans Memorial,

**[01:10:08.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000659)  
it's 61%.

**[01:10:11.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000660)  
But this does not reflect the

**[01:10:14.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000661)  
infrastructure that is going to be sourced

**[01:10:20.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000662)  
from the Office of the President.

**[01:10:23.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000663)  
And for these agencies,

**[01:10:26.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000664)  
they have one-time items.

**[01:10:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000665)  
Which for 2025,

**[01:10:31.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000666)  
which increase their budgets,

**[01:10:34.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000667)  
thus the reductions,

**[01:10:35.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000668)  
Your Honor.

**[01:10:36.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000669)  
And with the Veterans Memorial Medical Center,

**[01:10:39.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000670)  
they have trust receipts from the DOTR

**[01:10:42.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000671)  
because of the subway.

**[01:10:50.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000672)  
The AFP modernization,

**[01:10:53.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000673)  
Your Honor,

**[01:10:53.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000674)  
the proposal was 133.

**[01:10:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000676)  
Based on the re-Horizon 3 of the modernization program,

**[01:11:09.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000677)  
which has already,

**[01:11:11.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000678)  
it's in the process of being revised again

**[01:11:14.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000679)  
because of increased threats and increased costs.

**[01:11:19.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000680)  
So we once again will appeal,

**[01:11:23.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000681)  
and I think it's pending on the House

**[01:11:25.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000682)  
to already abolish the modernization,

**[01:11:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000683)  
modernization law.

**[01:11:28.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000684)  
Because we cannot work on 15-year horizons,

**[01:11:31.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000685)  
Your Honor,

**[01:11:32.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000686)  
which the modernization law provides.

**[01:11:36.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000687)  
It is not a sustainable way

**[01:11:40.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000688)  
of planning your modernization anymore.

**[01:11:46.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000689)  
In a more,

**[01:11:48.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000690)  
for lack of a better term,

**[01:11:50.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000691)  
but to use an economic term,

**[01:11:52.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000692)  
an opportunistic defense market.

**[01:11:57.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000693)  
It is a responsible protected environment.

**[01:12:00.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000694)  
We propose, Your Honor,

**[01:12:03.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000695)  
134 billion,

**[01:12:05.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000696)  
but only 50 billion was included.

**[01:12:08.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000697)  
I think the 50 billion,

**[01:12:10.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000698)  
Your Honor,

**[01:12:10.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000699)  
there is a sort of attachment

**[01:12:16.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000700)  
to the 50 billion amount

**[01:12:20.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000701)  
for the past how many years already?

**[01:12:24.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000702)  
And it was also due to the alignment,

**[01:12:27.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000703)  
perhaps of the revised AFP modernization to the basis conversion law and dividends from that.

**[01:12:38.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000704)  
But we know that the basis conversion BCDA has only remitted a total of 26 billion pesos

**[01:12:49.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000705)  
to the AFP for the past, no, I'm sorry, 45 billion for the past 26 years.

**[01:12:58.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000706)  
So it is not a sustainable way of going forward.

**[01:13:02.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000707)  
And yet it has taken away from us assets which cannot be replicated

**[01:13:09.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000708)  
because of their strategic location and strategic makeup.

**[01:13:14.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000709)  
So I believe that the,

**[01:13:19.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000710)  
the situation cannot be allowed to continue that more military reservations

**[01:13:27.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000711)  
which have to be devoted to national defense are thought of as commercial endeavors.

**[01:13:39.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000712)  
Because at the end of the day, it will not fund your modernization in any significant way at all.

**[01:13:48.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000713)  
As history has...

**[01:13:49.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000714)  
As history has proven.

**[01:13:54.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000715)  
So there are relatively few changes, Your Honor, for the rest of the items.

**[01:14:04.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000716)  
Next slide.

**[01:14:08.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000717)  
For the physical accomplishments, Your Honor, we accomplished 100 percent.

**[01:14:16.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000718)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:14:18.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000719)  
Of...

**[01:14:19.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000720)  
of D&D OSEC policies.

**[01:14:24.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000721)  
And I...

**[01:14:26.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000722)  
I also have to beg the indulgence of everybody here under our department

**[01:14:34.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000723)  
because any new policy causes a headache to them

**[01:14:37.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000724)  
and they would wish that I would not be churning out more and more policies anymore.

**[01:14:44.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000725)  
Sorry.

**[01:14:46.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000727)  
So 80 percent of those policies...

**[01:14:49.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000728)  
80 percent of those policies and strategies were accepted by the President and the Cabinet, Your Honor.

**[01:14:58.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000729)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:15:00.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000730)  
For the AFP, 81% of the tactical units provided force employers.

**[01:15:14.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000731)  
For the Philippine Army, Philippine Air Force 100%, and for the Philippine Navy, 100%, Your Honor.

**[01:15:22.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000732)  
However, I would like to point out that in the case of the Air Force and the Navy, the 100% is not a figure that, of course, for this year and last year, we should be very proud of, but we should be worried if it continues in the future because 100% is unsustainable.

**[01:15:46.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000733)  
That means to say, we are already at max capacity, if not less.

**[01:15:52.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000734)  
So, that means to say that the capacity of the agencies are not enough to sustain the needs of the environment, Your Honor, that we work in.

**[01:16:12.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000735)  
Continue, please.

**[01:16:16.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000736)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:16:17.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000737)  
On the Joint Force Planning Program.

**[01:16:22.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000738)  
Your Honor, 234 plans and programs were accomplished, and for the first semester of this year, there were 117 already accomplished.

**[01:16:35.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000739)  
Most of these, Your Honor, are for joint and combined anticipative actions, training exercises, scenario building, and scoping exercises.

**[01:16:52.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000740)  
On operations, Your Honor, the Joint Force Operations Program for operations, 159 were complied with last year out of 182.

**[01:17:07.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000741)  
And most of these plans were not, the unfulfilled portion was due to natural calamities.

**[01:17:20.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000742)  
Natural calamities.

**[01:17:22.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000743)  
That would, of course, necessitate the diversion of the resources to humanitarian assistance and disaster response.

**[01:17:36.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000744)  
This year, Your Honor, we have already completed 54% of our targets as of the first semester.

**[01:17:44.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000745)  
However, at the end of the year, we expect to complete 100%.

**[01:17:50.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000746)  
Okay.

**[01:17:52.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000747)  
For security operations, of course, Your Honor, in connection with and in cooperation with our civilian law enforcement agencies, there are about 5,900 activities that were covered last year and this year, 3,000, the most important of which are the ASEAN series of engagements.

**[01:18:13.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000748)  
For capability programs, Your Honor, the patients treated by the AFP Medical Center and other station hospitals were 12,000.

**[01:18:29.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000749)  
Accomplishment was 11,000 as of 2025 with 12,000 targets while this year, Your Honor,

**[01:18:42.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000750)  
the

**[01:18:43.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000751)  
since there was a gap last year, we reduced the target level to 10,000.

**[01:18:52.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000752)  
We have already had an accomplishment of more than 50%.

**[01:18:58.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000753)  
Ten signed AFP modernization projects are being implemented, so we have 60%, six have already been accomplished for this year.

**[01:19:12.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000754)  
Last year,

**[01:19:13.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000755)  
last year, there were 10 targets, Your Honor, we accomplished 17 because of the previous year's unfulfilled targets.

**[01:19:29.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000756)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:19:34.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000757)  
Matumal yung atin.

**[01:19:38.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000758)  
Natutulog yung atin.

**[01:19:39.800] DND Assistant Secretary Romeo N. Dela Cruz** (asr-000759)  
Actually,

**[01:19:41.180] DND Assistant Secretary Romeo N. Dela Cruz** (asr-000760)  
I want to...

**[01:19:42.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000761)  
I...

**[01:19:43.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000762)  
Naghang pala yung iyo.

**[01:19:44.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000763)  
Hindi ko mabasa yun.

**[01:19:47.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000764)  
Naghang yung iyo.

**[01:19:50.380] DND Assistant Secretary Romeo N. Dela Cruz** (asr-000765)  
Yes, sir.

**[01:19:55.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000766)  
For the government arsenal, Your Honor, 98% is the target this year.

**[01:20:03.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000767)  
Last year, we accomplished only 63% of the target because of the unavailability of propellants,

**[01:20:10.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000768)  
the shortage of propellants,

**[01:20:12.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000769)  
and other critical components in the international market.

**[01:20:17.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000770)  
The situation, Your Honor, in the international market now, it is a seller's market.

**[01:20:22.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000771)  
And even though you have fixed contracts, they will willingly pay the liquidated damages and other punitive amounts because of the amounts being offered by other users.

**[01:20:41.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000772)  
Given the several conflicts that are occurring now, both in the Middle East and in Ukraine.

**[01:20:50.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000773)  
Defense and security leaders capacity, Your Honor, 100% of our research papers have been accepted in the same target this year.

**[01:21:02.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000774)  
33% of 53 senior leaders.

**[01:21:11.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000775)  
This is from the DND and AAP.

**[01:21:15.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000776)  
And may I correct this too?

**[01:21:17.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000777)  
It's not only senior leaders.

**[01:21:20.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000778)  
Well, we can say senior leaders too because we opened up the MNSA program to enlisted personnel on the NCO level

**[01:21:29.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000779)  
because their leadership and their critical skills and critical knowledge is just as important as our officer corps.

**[01:21:41.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000780)  
And, Your Honor, there is a growth challenge with the NDCP now

**[01:21:48.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000781)  
because we are in the process of reshaping the curriculum.

**[01:22:00.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000782)  
Knowing fully well that the national security is multidimensional.

**[01:22:05.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000783)  
So, a fixed paradigm of national security as an academic.

**[01:22:11.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000784)  
And therefore, will not help.

**[01:22:15.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000785)  
Therefore, we continue with faculty development, advancing coordination and cooperation with other high-level research agencies, security agencies.

**[01:22:28.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000786)  
Probably, the national security paradigm before worked in an internal security environment.

**[01:22:34.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000787)  
But nowadays, with the different dimensions of conflict.

**[01:22:41.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000788)  
To include cyber conflict and cognitive warfare and who knows what will happen in the future.

**[01:22:47.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000789)  
It is not anticipative enough.

**[01:22:51.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000790)  
The academic output of the NDCP.

**[01:22:59.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000791)  
The teacher to student ratio is still 1 is to 10.

**[01:23:03.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000792)  
But I still want to know the research output of the institution.

**[01:23:09.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000793)  
And that is what I keep on putting.

**[01:23:11.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000794)  
And that is what I keep on pushing them to be doing.

**[01:23:13.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000795)  
Because if they do not publish, they do not produce peer-reviewed publications,

**[01:23:24.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000796)  
then we are not passing our job.

**[01:23:28.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000797)  
We don't pass the test.

**[01:23:34.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000798)  
So, next slide.

**[01:23:39.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000800)  
On OCD, Your Honor.

**[01:23:41.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000801)  
As we said, that 65% of the, we are concentrating right now on response.

**[01:23:57.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000802)  
And we also had to change the view of the minimum required standards for local governments.

**[01:24:07.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000803)  
For example, the Gawad Kalinga Awards.

**[01:24:11.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000804)  
They were even awarding before, awards for more than compliant.

**[01:24:19.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000805)  
But how can you say you're more than compliant when disaster risk reduction is a continuing endeavor?

**[01:24:26.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000806)  
And actually, how can you be more compliant with, for example, an earthquake scenario which nobody knows the outcome?

**[01:24:36.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000807)  
So, a more than compliant rating to me is impossible.

**[01:24:40.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000808)  
So, we abolished that standard and asked them to refocus on the ability to coordinate,

**[01:24:53.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000809)  
to exercise leadership in coordination with the different departments and the local government units.

**[01:25:01.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000810)  
So, even the training for first responders, Your Honor,

**[01:25:09.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000811)  
it should be on a classroom.

**[01:25:10.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000812)  
It should be on a clustered basis as we see the disasters now.

**[01:25:14.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000813)  
Because the paradigm of first responder coming from the affected community,

**[01:25:21.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000814)  
it does not hold water right now.

**[01:25:25.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000815)  
Because how can they respond when two or three municipalities are subject to stress at the same time?

**[01:25:35.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000816)  
So, it needs a different approach.

**[01:25:38.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000817)  
And I give an example.

**[01:25:40.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000818)  
For example, for the Ligtas Pinoy Act, where each town should have an evacuation center.

**[01:25:46.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000819)  
We had a very difficult time right now identifying if that is feasible or not.

**[01:25:53.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000820)  
Each town should have an evacuation center.

**[01:25:58.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000821)  
But in an area, perhaps even outside the physical boundaries of that town,

**[01:26:05.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000822)  
which can support the evacuees.

**[01:26:10.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000823)  
So, we had the first list of approved areas for the Ligtas Pinoy centers.

**[01:26:17.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000824)  
But it will be a continuing challenge for us to identify suitable areas that can accept the quantity of evacuees

**[01:26:30.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000825)  
should a disaster affect one area.

**[01:26:36.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000826)  
An example would be in the Negros area.

**[01:26:40.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000827)  
Ashfall, Your Honor.

**[01:26:42.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000828)  
About four or five municipalities were affected.

**[01:26:46.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000829)  
But the only safe evacuation center would be in another distant municipality.

**[01:26:52.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000830)  
The same area, the same situation with Bicol, when you have a six-kilometer exclusion zone.

**[01:27:00.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000831)  
So, that is a dimension which we are working with.

**[01:27:05.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000832)  
And we will also report the results to Congress.

**[01:27:10.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000833)  
To refine whatever modifications are needed, Your Honor.

**[01:27:17.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000834)  
The rest of the accomplishments, Your Honor, we met 100% of the targets.

**[01:27:27.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000835)  
Next slide.

**[01:27:30.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000836)  
For PIVAW, Your Honor, on the veterans' welfare and benefits.

**[01:27:36.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000837)  
I'll take that, yeah.

**[01:27:38.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000838)  
100%.

**[01:27:40.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000839)  
100% of the target was accomplished because these are regular pensions.

**[01:27:47.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000840)  
And it is only in the efficiency of the payment.

**[01:27:53.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000841)  
On the veterans' affairs management programs,

**[01:28:10.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000842)  
still, it is a question, Your Honor, of getting the number of bona fide accredited veterans

**[01:28:18.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000843)  
or their dependents.

**[01:28:22.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000844)  
So, 57% of the target was accomplished.

**[01:28:31.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000845)  
This is of particular importance, Your Honor,

**[01:28:33.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000846)  
because of a recent phenomenon that,

**[01:28:37.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000847)  
we worked with, with the U.S. government.

**[01:28:43.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000848)  
The U.S. government recently gave a grant to the Veterans Memorial Medical Center

**[01:28:48.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000849)  
because of the prevalence yet of,

**[01:28:56.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000850)  
no, the presence, not prevalence,

**[01:28:58.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000851)  
the presence of tuberculosis among our veterans.

**[01:29:04.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000852)  
We are able only to track,

**[01:29:06.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000853)  
600,

**[01:29:09.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000854)  
six per,

**[01:29:10.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000855)  
how many,

**[01:29:13.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000856)  
there is a gap of 2,000, I believe,

**[01:29:18.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000857)  
that we need to track.

**[01:29:21.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000858)  
And this will only happen, Your Honor,

**[01:29:24.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000859)  
if the PIVAW is adequately tracking our veterans.

**[01:29:31.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000860)  
So, this PIVAW,

**[01:29:34.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000861)  
rather than have,

**[01:29:36.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000862)  
recognition, ceremonies,

**[01:29:39.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000863)  
which are also important,

**[01:29:41.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000864)  
they need to go out in the field right now

**[01:29:44.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000865)  
to scope the number of veterans

**[01:29:46.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000866)  
and to bring them within the VMMC environment

**[01:29:50.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000867)  
so we can adequately track their wellness and their health care.

**[01:29:56.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000868)  
And they have also been tasked to work with

**[01:30:00.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000869)  
the VMMC to

**[01:30:01.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000870)  
build hospices, Your Honor,

**[01:30:04.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000871)  
where our veterans can

**[01:30:06.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000872)

**[01:30:08.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000873)  
A lot of them,

**[01:30:10.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000874)  
as Senator

**[01:30:11.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000875)  
Lacson knows,

**[01:30:14.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000876)  
have been

**[01:30:15.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000877)  
constrained

**[01:30:17.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000878)  
to have an extended

**[01:30:19.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000879)  
period of stay in the hospitals,

**[01:30:22.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000880)  
although

**[01:30:23.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000881)  
they do not need medical

**[01:30:25.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000882)  
care, but they need

**[01:30:26.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000883)

**[01:30:33.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000884)

**[01:30:35.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000885)

**[01:30:37.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000886)

**[01:30:39.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000887)  
it will be heartless for

**[01:30:41.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000888)

**[01:30:43.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000889)  
VMMC to discharge

**[01:30:45.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000890)

**[01:30:47.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000891)  
So the

**[01:30:48.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000892)  
PIVA was tasked to make this

**[01:30:51.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000893)  
a priority.

**[01:30:53.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000894)  
We have also

**[01:30:55.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000895)  
seen

**[01:30:56.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000896)  
and I have witnessed

**[01:30:58.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000897)  
personally retired

**[01:31:01.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000898)  
officers

**[01:31:02.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000899)  
who, because of

**[01:31:05.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000900)  
the detachment from military

**[01:31:07.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000901)  
life, have

**[01:31:09.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000902)  
lost their

**[01:31:10.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000903)  
social circle.

**[01:31:13.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000904)  
And a lot of them who

**[01:31:15.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000905)  
have been afflicted by

**[01:31:16.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000906)
