**[01:31:19.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000907)  
withdraw and

**[01:31:21.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000908)  
become

**[01:31:21.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000909)  
you know

**[01:31:24.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000910)  
a

**[01:31:26.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000911)  
are affected and afflicted

**[01:31:28.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000912)  
with self-pity,

**[01:31:30.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000913)  
loss of morale.

**[01:31:32.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000914)  
So they also need

**[01:31:33.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000915)  
their social

**[01:31:36.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000916)  
environment

**[01:31:36.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000917)  
to be able to mingle

**[01:31:40.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000918)  
and to keep their mental

**[01:31:42.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000919)  
wellness intact.

**[01:31:44.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000920)  
So this in

**[01:31:46.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000921)  
connection with the hospices

**[01:31:47.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000922)  
so that we can also support

**[01:31:50.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000923)  
them that

**[01:31:51.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000924)  
in the

**[01:31:53.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000925)  
most vulnerable period of their

**[01:31:56.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000926)  
time when they need us

**[01:31:58.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000927)  
most, we can preserve their

**[01:32:00.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000928)  
dignity, your honor, just like

**[01:32:02.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000929)  
they do in Korea for that matter.

**[01:32:08.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000930)  
Next slide.

**[01:32:12.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000931)  
So for veterans, your honor,

**[01:32:14.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000932)  
the accomplishment

**[01:32:16.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000933)

**[01:32:19.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000934)  
treated, but this is only 6%

**[01:32:21.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000935)  
because it is in Manila.

**[01:32:24.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000936)  
And the design now,

**[01:32:26.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000937)  
there is a veterans health service

**[01:32:28.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000938)  
that's being

**[01:32:29.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000939)  
before it was

**[01:32:32.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000940)

That situation is untenable

**[01:32:53.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000951)  
So it does not serve the

**[01:32:56.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000952)

**[01:32:57.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000953)  
So we refocus

**[01:32:59.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-000954)  
the programs and plans

to have

first and how we can

So these targets

and with the

There are three already

So that they do not need

If the public hospitals

We have three and the reason

for three alone, your honor,

So we continue

the recruitment drive

and to establish them

where we can

For 2026,

**[01:36:01.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001023)  
the first semester, your honor,

**[01:36:06.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001024)  
the appropriation for this year

**[01:36:09.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001025)  
was 489.

**[01:36:12.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001026)

Disbursements, 163

**[01:36:20.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001029)  
for the first semester.

**[01:36:25.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001031)

**[01:36:42.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001034)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:36:48.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001035)  
For comparatives, Your Honor,

**[01:36:51.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001036)  
which is the source also of the year-on-year,

**[01:36:54.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001037)  
which probably does not reflect current needs.

**[01:37:01.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001038)  
What is important here is we will see, Your Honor,

**[01:37:05.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001039)  
that there is an increasing trend of both obligations and allotments.

**[01:37:16.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001040)  
Our challenge during the start, Your Honor,

**[01:37:19.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001041)  
was the low approved budget system

**[01:37:24.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001042)  
and the feelings for items that we needed to acquire.

**[01:37:34.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001043)  
And with the inflation catching up, particularly for defense material,

**[01:37:40.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001044)  
we could not obligate the amounts.

**[01:37:45.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001045)  
And it would have been a waste of taxpayers' money to do so.

**[01:37:49.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001046)  
And I discourage the practice of...

**[01:37:54.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001047)  
rushing to obligate just to satisfy the 100% obligation rate.

**[01:38:02.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001048)  
I felt that, you know, let's just give a good reason why it was not obligated.

**[01:38:11.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001049)  
And we refuse to obligate.

**[01:38:13.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001050)  
I particularly refuse to obligate if we're just going to end up with something that is...

**[01:38:21.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001051)  
is...

**[01:38:22.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001052)  
is...

**[01:38:24.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001053)  
something not optimal for us.

**[01:38:29.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001054)  
But it has...

**[01:38:32.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001055)  
particularly for the 2024 and 2025,

**[01:38:39.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001056)  
when there have been very major changes in the nature of the equipment that we need.

**[01:38:48.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001057)  
And we had significant lessons learned...

**[01:38:54.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001058)  
for the supportability and integration and interoperability of platforms.

**[01:39:00.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001059)  
So we required full sustainment to come with acquisitions.

**[01:39:09.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001060)  
And most importantly, guaranteed supply chains and guaranteed interoperability and upgrades.

**[01:39:18.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001061)  
Plus, we required a reserve of at least 30 days of munitions.

**[01:39:23.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001062)  
We...

**[01:39:24.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001063)  
and every platform that...

**[01:39:27.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001064)  
we notionally will acquire.

**[01:39:32.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001065)  
We also had the experience or honor of purchasing bare platforms and the weaponry to follow.

**[01:39:42.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001066)  
I do not think that is a proper way to go, which was why we stopped the practice.

**[01:39:47.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001067)  
However, this results in increased acquisition costs.

**[01:39:54.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001068)  
beforehand because as we know prices just keep on going up so you might as well lock it in

**[01:40:03.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001069)  
at one time and get the whole package guaranteed

**[01:40:10.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001070)  
this also has resulted in the abc's being and being too low for us so it is a continuing

**[01:40:23.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001071)  
challenge your honor with the uh with the greater costs of uh the essential platforms we need

**[01:40:32.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001072)  
of course we have also been investing in mitigation hybrids and you know evolutionary

**[01:40:40.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001073)  
uh platforms however this is part and parcel of a mix that you will need to have

**[01:40:49.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001074)  
once again i reiterate our gaps are infrastructure

**[01:40:54.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001075)  
basic platforms and

**[01:41:00.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001076)  
logistics facilities and support facilities that we will need on a resilient basis

**[01:41:09.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001077)  
next slide

**[01:41:12.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001078)  
issues and concerns your honor uh next slide

**[01:41:19.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001079)  
uh the uh 21 line items we

**[01:41:23.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001080)  
we submitted just to complete programs only three have been approved

**[01:41:31.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001081)  
but this is reflective your honor of the

**[01:41:43.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001082)  
consideration of infrastructure that we need to underscore given the fact that

**[01:41:50.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001083)  
uh we were deprived of

**[01:41:58.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001084)  
infrastructure that were designed really for military use that have been converted

**[01:42:04.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001085)  
So, the unfunded, Your Honor, comes out to about 87, but I do not know after the House, Your Honor, would have submitted to the Senate their version, how much of this would have been answered for.

**[01:42:30.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001086)  
Next slide.

**[01:42:31.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001087)  
For the modernization, Your Honor, minimum of 70 just for re-Horizon 3, but we should see there is still Horizon 2 projects that need to be completed, but we also scrapped, repurposed some of them because a lot of Horizon 2 projects are already obsolete.

**[01:43:01.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001088)  
But this does not include, we have not yet come out, Your Honor, with the recalibration of Horizon 3, which we need to submit to the Senate for the President for approval.

**[01:43:17.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001089)  
The program of 50 billion, Your Honor.

**[01:43:31.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001090)  
If we obligate and contract out all of these, we will need 20 billion more from the current 50 billion that is being proposed just to pay for the continuing multi-year continuing obligational authority, which is why also our obligation rate is low.

**[01:44:01.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001091)  
If we obligate for that year, our payments would balloon and it will be beyond the modernization.

**[01:44:31.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001092)  
Next slide, Your Honor.

**[01:44:41.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001093)  
With the indulgence of the Committee, I will move to the next slide, Your Honor.

**[01:44:56.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001094)  
This one, Your Honor, we already included it last year.

**[01:45:00.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001095)  
In the absence of the amendatory law for the presidential decree, Your Honor, limiting foreign borrowings for defense to 300 million U.S. dollars.

**[01:45:18.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001096)  
We have a bill already pending, Your Honor, to take that limitation out.

**[01:45:25.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001097)  
It allows contracting for foreign loans, grants, and other credit facilities.

**[01:45:37.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001098)  
However, under the control of the Secretary of Finance, Your Honor, we fully understand that this has to be subject to fiscal space limitations.

**[01:45:48.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001099)  
And we fully accept the

**[01:45:55.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001100)  
seniority and the primacy of the Secretary of Finance in these matters.

**[01:46:01.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001101)  
So it has been enacted last year.

**[01:46:04.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001102)  
We did not avail of it, Your Honor, because of the mechanism with the Secretary of Finance.

**[01:46:12.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001103)  
So we appeal for the same flexibility to be enacted this year.

**[01:46:23.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001104)  
In anticipation also, Your Honor.

**[01:46:25.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001105)  
In anticipation also, Your Honor, of the repeal of the 300 million dollar limitation.

**[01:46:34.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001106)  
It is a continuing provision, Your Honor.

**[01:46:38.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001107)  
And we have not availed of it because knowing fiscal space limitations.

**[01:46:49.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001108)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:46:52.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001109)  
This is the last slide, Your Honor.

**[01:46:53.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001110)

From Your Honors.

Thank you, Secretary Teodoro.

**[01:47:04.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001113)  
But I will ask my colleagues to go and propound questions first.

**[01:47:09.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001114)  
But before them, meron lang ano, may patanong lang yung ano, naghahabol kasi mag-media natin.

**[01:47:14.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001115)  
Deadline, no?

**[01:47:15.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001116)  
Singit lang ako ng dalawa.

**[01:47:17.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001117)  
Sec, habang nagpe-present kayo ng budget kanina, lumabas sa balita.

**[01:47:21.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001118)  
Now, allow me to quote the statement of the DFA Secretary,

**[01:47:24.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001119)  
the Department of Foreign Affairs.

**[01:47:26.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001120)  
Summoned Chinese Ambassador Jing Kuan on October 19 to convey the Philippine serious concern and strong objection.

**[01:47:33.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001121)  
This October 15 public statement which conveyed the threat to use of force.

**[01:47:37.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001122)  
So ano ba ang treatment ng DND sa mga ganitong statement ng isang foreign diplomat?

**[01:47:42.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001123)  
I know that both of us are subject to ano now, no?

**[01:47:47.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001124)  
Being attacked by, I don't know kung itong mga ito, mga tools to.

**[01:47:53.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001125)  
I don't know.

**[01:47:53.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001126)  
But.

**[01:47:54.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001127)  
We are both, we just want to make, we just want to make it clear.

**[01:47:58.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001128)  
But that both of us, me and myself and my, the Secretary [unclear], we both studied in Xavier School, both studied Chinese.

**[01:48:05.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001129)  
And I'm sure that he, we love our Chinese schoolmates and classmates.

**[01:48:11.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001130)  
So it is not the Chinese people just to be straight at the, to set the record straight.

**[01:48:18.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001131)  
It's the expansionist.

**[01:48:24.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001132)  
It's the stock of Xi Jinping's government.

**[01:48:27.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001133)  
So probably, I'm sure just quickly, Secretary Guibo, how does the DND treat this kind of public statement that convey a threat of use of force from the Chinese ambassador?

**[01:48:38.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001134)  
Your Honor, consistent with our earlier pronouncements, number one, we refute all allegations made by the ambassador that we are troublemakers and are provoking.

**[01:48:53.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001135)  
We rate.

**[01:48:54.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001136)  
We reiterate, nulit namin, Your Honor, ang aming pag-suporta at pag-taguyod sa UNCLOS and the UN Charter.

**[01:49:09.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001137)  
Pangalawa, pinahiwatig na po natin ang ating pag-object sa kanyang pananalita.

**[01:49:18.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001138)  
Kasi hindi lang naman isang beses sinabi niyang you lose.

**[01:49:23.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001139)  
Yung pinoportray po ngayon.

**[01:49:24.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001140)  
Ay yung we can use force if we want, but we may win easily.

**[01:49:32.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001141)  
Meron pong isa na sinabi siya na okay, you cut military communications, but you lose.

**[01:49:42.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001142)  
So ibig po sabihin, may pagbabantana po doon.

**[01:49:48.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001143)  
Dalawang beses po niyang sinabi po yan.

**[01:49:53.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001144)  
Ngayon po.

**[01:49:54.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001145)  
Pinatawag na po siya ng DFA, we have to wait, Your Honor, for developments which, of course, the President or the DFA will message to the public at an appropriate time.

**[01:50:14.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001146)  
Pero maliwanag po ang ating pagtutol, hindi lang dito sa statement niya, kung hindi, the more important thing, yung kanilang false narrative.

**[01:50:24.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001147)  
May nine-dash line, may meron silang jurisdiction, pangatlo, yung kanila pong latest narrative na ang Batanes ay part ng China.

**[01:50:39.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001148)  
Kasi meron na daw silang karapatan na mag-operate sa mga karagatan na nagpapalibot sa Batanes.

**[01:50:49.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001149)  
Yan po ay matinding tinututulan at aming pong sinusubukan.

**[01:50:54.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001150)  
Na i-deter yung ganyang klaseng mga behavior.

**[01:50:58.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001151)  
Salamat po.

**[01:51:00.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001152)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[01:51:02.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001153)

**[01:51:08.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001154)  
And we believe in diplomacy, but we also believe in developing good relations with our neighbors.

**[01:51:13.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001155)  
Pero siyempre, dapat may respeto sa isa't isa.

**[01:51:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001156)  
Medyo hindi na maganda ang kanyang mga aksyon at mga pananalita

**[01:51:24.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001157)  
na may halong pananakot.

**[01:51:30.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001158)  
Very hostile and very undiplomatic.

**[01:51:34.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001159)  
And we would like to support our Secretary also.

**[01:51:38.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001160)  
Your stand also when you were in Singapore.

**[01:51:42.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001161)

**[01:51:48.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001162)  
And we are just protecting our sovereignty and our territorial integrity.

**[01:51:53.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001163)  
So...

**[01:51:54.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001164)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[01:51:55.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001165)

**[01:51:56.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001166)

**[01:52:09.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001169)

**[01:52:24.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001172)  
His questions and concerns.

**[01:52:26.240] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001174)  
Thank you, Mr. President and Mr. Chairman.

**[01:52:29.200] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001175)  
Yung isang item dito, yung unused appropriations.

**[01:52:32.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001176)  
Of course, when we speak of unused appropriations, it is the total of unobligated at saka unreleased.

**[01:52:40.020] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001177)  
For 2024, pag pinagsama yung dalawa, nasa mga 32 billion.

**[01:52:49.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001178)  
32 million.

**[01:52:50.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001179)  
Yeah.

**[01:52:51.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001180)  
Billion.

**[01:52:51.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001181)  
32 billion.

**[01:52:53.580] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001182)  
2022.

**[01:52:54.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001183)  
Wala na ito kasi under the continuing appropriations, 2 years lang eh.

**[01:52:59.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001184)  
In 2025, nasa mga 31 billion.

**[01:53:03.140] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001185)  
Again, unobligated and unreleased.

**[01:53:06.640] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001186)  
So what would account for the...

**[01:53:11.280] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001187)  
I think this is huge numbers, 31 billion in unused appropriations for 2025.

**[01:53:20.080] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001188)  
What would account, sir,

**[01:53:21.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001188-split-2)  
are the

**[01:53:22.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001189)  
inflationary pressures dun sa mga kinakailangan po natin i-acquire.

**[01:53:28.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001190)  
At dun naman po sa limited na capital outlay, dahil po sa costings ng materials, umaakyat po yung halaga.

**[01:53:41.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001191)  
Kaya po, kung...

**[01:53:43.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001192)  
Pangantlo po, yung pag ginamit yung ibang items po dun sa unobligated,

**[01:53:52.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001193)  
pag inobligate po, tataas.

**[01:53:54.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001194)  
Tapos pa po yung MICA natin sa amount ng modernization.

**[01:53:58.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001195)  
So, yung fiscal constraints po, and yung pag-costing po ng mga horizons,

**[01:54:08.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001196)  
gumagapang po yung presyo halos araw-araw.

**[01:54:11.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001197)  
So, pag may nakita...

**[01:54:15.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001198)  
Unang-una po, baka dun po sa approved bid ceiling kasi,

**[01:54:19.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001199)  
doon po sa mga reglamento natin,

**[01:54:22.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001200)  
hindi na po...

**[01:54:24.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001201)  
ayun sa presyo na gusto natin.

**[01:54:30.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001202)  
Pangalawa po, yung ating pong patakaran na kailangan po yung platforms are fully weaponized

**[01:54:40.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001203)  
and provided with reserves kasi yun po ang nakikita.

**[01:54:45.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001204)  
Actually, very, very conservative yung reserves natin na 30 days na munitions per platform.

**[01:54:51.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001205)  
Pero alam po natin na humahaba.

**[01:54:54.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001206)  
At hindi na po yung panahon na kailangan po natin na reserba.

**[01:54:58.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001207)  
Yun po ang primary reason po from 2024.

**[01:55:02.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001208)  
Because I remember, at the end of 2023 po,

**[01:55:06.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001209)  
doon pumasok ang ReHorizon 3.

**[01:55:08.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001210)  
Kasi nag-revisa po kami ng kinakailangan, kaya nagbago po ito.

**[01:55:14.300] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001211)  
So, the next relevant question would be,

**[01:55:18.100] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001212)  
how do we resolve this issue?

**[01:55:19.840] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001213)  
Kasi year in, year out,

**[01:55:21.600] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001214)  
ito lagi yung nagiging problem.

**[01:55:24.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001215)  
We're trying to catch up, but it seems that we are not catching up.

**[01:55:28.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001216)  
Sir, there should really be a determination to have continuous defense spending increases

**[01:55:36.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001217)  
which will impact the total fiscal picture of the government.

**[01:55:43.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001218)  
I will give an example, Your Honor.

**[01:55:45.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001219)  
Ang Singapore from day one ng panganak niya in 1965 or 66,

**[01:55:53.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001220)  
6%.

**[01:55:54.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001221)  
ng GDP at the minimum ang ginagastos po nila.

**[01:55:59.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001222)  
Tayo po ay sa core defense spending, wala pa po sa dos.

**[01:56:05.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001223)  
Nasasakal na po yung budget natin.

**[01:56:08.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001224)  
I think it should be a determination, Your Honor,

**[01:56:14.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001225)  
na we raise the amount through financing

**[01:56:18.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001226)  
because if we make a determination,

**[01:56:21.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001227)  
nagagastusin lang talaga ito.

**[01:56:23.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001228)  
On the future,

**[01:56:24.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001229)  
for generations,

**[01:56:25.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001230)  
mabuti pa,

**[01:56:27.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001231)  
umpisahan na natin ang tama sa ngayon

**[01:56:30.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001232)  
kesa sa pamahal ng pamahal o pasikip ng pasikip ito.

**[01:56:34.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001233)  
So, I think it is a macroeconomic problem

**[01:56:38.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001234)  
that has to be solved macroeconomically.

**[01:56:42.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001235)  
Pangalawa naman po,

**[01:56:44.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001236)  
nile-leverage din po natin ang alliances natin

**[01:56:47.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001237)  
katulad ng official security assistance ng Japan

**[01:56:51.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001238)  
na 200 million kada taon na US dollars.

**[01:56:54.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001239)  
At FMF financing sa US

**[01:56:57.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001240)  
at sa iba pa pong partners sa Australia at sa Canada in the future.

**[01:57:03.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001241)  
Pero hindi ito substitute po talaga.

**[01:57:08.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001242)  
And I see your point, Your Honor.

**[01:57:10.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001243)  
I think it will go beyond my mandate

**[01:57:15.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001244)  
to view a solution to this problem.

**[01:57:21.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001245)  
The acquisition,

**[01:57:24.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001246)  
the avenues of getting the funds,

**[01:57:29.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001247)  
we can probably remediate,

**[01:57:32.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001248)  
but the repayment is the problem

**[01:57:35.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001249)  
which transcends the D&D.

**[01:57:40.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001250)  
We are really reflecting the realities

**[01:57:44.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001251)  
of the costs that we need.

**[01:57:49.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001252)  
At the end of the day,

**[01:57:50.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001253)  
it's a political decision

**[01:57:51.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001254)  
whether you fund this

**[01:57:53.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001255)  
versus,

**[01:57:53.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001256)  
versus something else.

**[01:57:55.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001257)  
Because it's really a zero-sum game.

**[01:57:57.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001258)  
There's no denying it.

**[01:58:03.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001259)  
That's as best as I can answer

**[01:58:05.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001260)  
our Vice Chairperson.

**[01:58:08.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001261)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:58:10.560] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001262)  
Before I move on to another topic,

**[01:58:12.300] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001263)  
I'd like to find out from COA,

**[01:58:14.060] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001264)  
are there major procurement issues

**[01:58:17.280] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001265)  
that should merit our attention

**[01:58:20.520] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001266)  
regarding the D&D?

**[01:58:23.580] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001267)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:58:26.540] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001268)  
Thank you.

**[01:58:27.460] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001269)  
Good afternoon, Sir, Your Honor.

**[01:58:29.260] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001270)  
For the D&D OSEC, Sir,

**[01:58:31.880] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001271)  
in which I am the supervising...

**[01:58:33.060] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001272)  
Not just the OSEC,

**[01:58:34.020] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001273)  
the establishment.

**[01:58:37.140] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001274)  
May I call on the other

**[01:58:38.800] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001275)

**[01:58:40.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001276)
