**[01:58:42.300] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001277)  
For the Armed Forces of the Philippines,

**[01:58:46.220] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001278)  
the General Headquarters.

**[01:58:49.340] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001279)  
May I?

**[01:58:49.660] COA Supervising Auditor Rosana A. Alconcel** (asr-001280)  
Thank you.

**[01:58:53.580] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001281)  
Kaya lang ako concerned kasi so many years ago, same budget deliberation sa DND, meron kami nakitang COA issue na 10 years na hindi pa na-deliver, laging in transit.

**[01:59:09.600] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001282)  
So ang tanong ni Senator Enrile noon, ang tagal naman mag-deliver yan, 10 years pa. Every year, recurring yung issue being raised by COA. So hindi na na-deliver sa end user. So these are the issues that I'm talking about. Kung meron mga parallel or similar issues na dapat i-attend or maybe pwede nang burahin sa libro nyo kasi 10 years, hindi pa dumarating eh.

**[01:59:39.600] COA Supervising Auditor Norina Rose L. Yao** (asr-001283)  
Good afternoon everyone, Your Honor. Regarding po dun sa deliveries, wala naman po tayong question about po dun sa actual delivery but more on documentation. And ongoing naman po yung retrieval ng mga documents. Yun nga po nandun po tayo sa 10 years.

**[02:00:00.000] COA Supervising Auditor Norina Rose L. Yao** (asr-001284)  
But, however, yung ongoing coordination naman po is currently conducted po.

**[02:00:05.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001285)  
Any other specific issues that you can share with us, with the committee?

**[02:00:09.640] COA Supervising Auditor Norina Rose L. Yao** (asr-001286)  
Ano naman?

**[02:00:10.540] COA Supervising Auditor Norina Rose L. Yao** (asr-001287)  
Ano po.

**[02:00:11.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001288)  
So, totoo yan ah?

**[02:00:12.860] COA Supervising Auditor Norina Rose L. Yao** (asr-001289)  
Yes po.

**[02:00:13.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001290)  
Okay.

**[02:00:14.400] COA Supervising Auditor Norina Rose L. Yao** (asr-001291)  
Thank you.

**[02:00:16.600] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001292)  
Doon sa malay na foreign influence and interference, yung security sector, meron ba tayong data and substantiated reports?

**[02:00:28.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001293)  
Of actual malign interference by foreign jurisdictions against our country?

**[02:00:35.680] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001294)  
Considering na hindi pa natin ipasa yung amendment ng decades-old anti-espionage law?

**[02:00:43.480] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001295)  
So, how do we confront this kind of a situation?

**[02:00:47.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001296)  
Sir, we can only confront it now if there is a predicate crime that has been committed.

**[02:00:57.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001297)  
Or is...

**[02:00:58.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001298)  
It's in the process of being committed.

**[02:01:00.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001299)  
And what we have seen so far is on the economic sabotage aspect, like money laundering, human trafficking aspect also, Your Honor.

**[02:01:14.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001300)  
And environmental standards plus fake citizenships.

**[02:01:26.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001301)  
However, on the...

**[02:01:28.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001302)  
Activities, actually, sir, that will be maligned, we have information.

**[02:01:37.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001303)  
However, substantiated information is quite difficult given the constraints that we have.

**[02:01:47.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001304)  
Particularly for the connections between influencer and influence.

**[02:01:58.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001305)  
Political influence, ano?

**[02:02:00.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001306)  
Political, sir, and others.

**[02:02:01.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001307)  
That's part of the malign interference, eh?

**[02:02:03.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001308)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:02:04.300] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001309)  
Yung covert operations na influencing, you know, even the mga GNC, hindi ba?

**[02:02:13.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001310)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:02:14.120] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001311)  
Through social media and all.

**[02:02:15.460] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001312)  
Now, what has happened to the arrests conducted by, I think, the NBI, you know?

**[02:02:22.980] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001313)  
By foreign agents conducting surveillance?

**[02:02:28.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001314)  
Surveillance operations sa near Camp Aguinaldo, and then also sa Comelec.

**[02:02:32.620] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001315)  
We asked this during the first committee hearing of the anti-espionage law, yung bill.

**[02:02:38.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001316)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:02:39.620] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001317)  
So, walang feedback, eh?

**[02:02:41.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001318)  
Sir, on the recent disruption operation, it was a Pogo operation.

**[02:02:50.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001319)  
We are still exploiting the recovery.

**[02:02:53.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001320)  
But the important thing is that the...

**[02:02:58.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001321)  
hub was closed down.

**[02:03:01.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001322)  
And about more or less a hundred were taken by the BI.

**[02:03:08.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001323)  
So, they are awaiting deportation proceedings.

**[02:03:16.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001324)  
I think deportation proceedings now are the most effective way of dealing with the persons apprehended.

**[02:03:27.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001325)  
Unless...

**[02:03:28.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001326)  
Yes, they are principals.

**[02:03:30.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001327)  
Because if they are subjected to judicial processes,

**[02:03:35.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001328)  
somehow, sometimes, they are able to continue the activities.

**[02:03:43.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001329)  
It's better that they are gone unless they are principals.

**[02:03:48.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001330)  
For example, Your Honor Alice Go,

**[02:03:51.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001331)  
which was a cut-and-dried case insofar as we're concerned.

**[02:03:55.320] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001332)  
It had nothing to do with the malign interference or...

**[02:03:58.120] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001333)  
Espionage operations.

**[02:03:59.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001334)  
We are still in the process, sir, of...

**[02:04:01.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001335)  
Hindi pa na-validate.

**[02:04:03.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001336)  
Hindi pa sir na-validate.

**[02:04:03.620] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001337)  
So far, Pogo yung nakita niyong motive.

**[02:04:05.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001338)  
So far, Pogo, sir.

**[02:04:07.280] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001339)  
Pero ang location niya nakakapagtaka.

**[02:04:10.600] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001340)  
Katulad, parang yung Pogo na na-disrupt sa likod ng DFA noong 2023 or 2024.

**[02:04:21.640] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001341)  
May analysis na doon sa equipment, surveillance equipment that were confiscated.

**[02:04:26.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001342)  
Ginagawa pa sa kasuluku yan, sir.

**[02:04:28.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001343)  
Matagal na yun, ah.

**[02:04:29.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001344)  
Last week lang yata, sir.

**[02:04:31.180] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001345)  
No, no. Recent. I think this was one or two years ago.

**[02:04:35.540] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001346)  
I was referring to the arrests made by, I think, NBI yata or PNP.

**[02:04:42.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001347)  
I'm not sure about that, Your Honor.

**[02:04:45.440] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001348)  
I think close coordination should be made with the Armed Forces Intelligence.

**[02:04:49.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001349)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:04:50.420] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001350)  
This could involve national security.

**[02:04:53.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001351)  
However, Your Honor, one constraint.

**[02:04:56.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001352)  
Assuming that there is...

**[02:04:58.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001353)  
Sometimes, what we have encountered in cases that we want to follow up is that to disrupt, to exploit a computer or a device would need a data warrant, another legal proceeding.

**[02:05:20.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001354)  
So, I will find out, Your Honor, about the status kasi marami na po eh.

**[02:05:26.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001355)  
Ang interesting...

**[02:05:28.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001356)  
Ang interesting thing po namin ay tumigil yung operation.

**[02:05:31.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001357)  
Katulad po nung ibang mga na-disrupt natin, hindi naman lang yung fake na or substandard na ginagawa.

**[02:05:40.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001358)  
Kung hindi may [unclear] ito eh, nalabas-pasok ang...

**[02:05:44.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001359)  
Matigil lang po yun.

**[02:05:47.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001360)  
Initially, ay one half of the problem solved.

**[02:05:51.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001361)  
Kasi hindi na pwedeng gamitin yung lugar nalabas-pasok ang kung sino.

**[02:05:58.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001362)  
So, about the devices, Your Honor, I will find out, Your Honor.

**[02:06:03.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001363)  
Those arrests that I was referring to, nangyari last year eh, near Comelec and in the vicinity of Camp Aguinaldo.

**[02:06:11.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001364)  
And that should tell us a lot, ano?

**[02:06:13.940] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001365)  
Kasi kung Camp Aguinaldo and these are supposed to be foreign agents, then that should be a major concern.

**[02:06:21.560] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001366)  
Threat sa national security.

**[02:06:24.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001367)  
We'll find out, Your Honor.

**[02:06:25.260] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001368)  
Yeah, please.

**[02:06:26.580] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001369)  
And please do something about it.

**[02:06:28.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001370)  
Because baka mamaya, may mga reports din tayo, may mga sleeper na, di ba?

**[02:06:34.140] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001371)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:06:34.740] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001372)  
In the country.

**[02:06:36.340] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001373)  
And this could be tied to the reports of late registration of birth.

**[02:06:42.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001374)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:06:43.520] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001375)  
Ang last count yata, nasa mga 3,000 ba o 5,000?

**[02:06:47.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001376)  
Sir, I'm not sure of the amount.

**[02:06:48.700] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001377)  
Identified, yeah.

**[02:06:49.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001378)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:06:51.320] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001379)  
Identified the personalities na late registration.

**[02:06:55.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001380)  
I don't know what's the status now.

**[02:06:58.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001381)  
Kung ano na ginawa ng PSA.

**[02:07:00.720] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001382)  
Tinanong rin namin sa PSA ito during the budget deliberation.

**[02:07:04.100] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001383)  
And they're trying to find some remedial measures.

**[02:07:08.400] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001384)  
But, you know, masyado malalim na pag ganyang karami.

**[02:07:12.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001385)  
Malalim na po talaga, sir.

**[02:07:13.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001386)  
At yung iba, second generation na eh.

**[02:07:16.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001387)  
Ang pasok dito ng magulang ay talagang, ano, yung anak naman ang pinag-late registration.

**[02:07:25.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001388)  
Di umano.

**[02:07:25.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001389)  
Si Alice Gu ay ganun din.

**[02:07:28.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001390)  
Pero, meron din nagsasabi na hindi nga ganun ang nangyari sa kanya.

**[02:07:36.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001391)  
Bagong pasok, bagong register.

**[02:07:42.100] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001392)  
Anyway, kindly look into it, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:07:45.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001393)  
Will do, sir.

**[02:07:46.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001394)  
And we'll report to you.

**[02:07:48.060] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001395)  
I will yield the floor to Senator Tulfo or the Senate President, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:07:55.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001396)  
Our Senate President.

**[02:07:56.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001397)  
Thank you.

**[02:07:57.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001398)  
Thank you.

**[02:07:58.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001399)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[02:08:00.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001400)  
I'll start off, Mr. Chairman, with some budgets, some of the items in the 2026 budget.

**[02:08:09.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001401)  
Na-discuss ni Secretary ito kanina, yung sa loan.

**[02:08:15.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001402)  
And I would like to understand how come the special provision wasn't triggered.

**[02:08:22.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001403)  
I think it was...

**[02:08:24.760] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001404)  
Nalagay natin itong special provision in foreign...

**[02:08:28.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001405)  
and domestic financing arrangements.

**[02:08:30.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001406)  
This is an offshoot from an information from the Coast Guard.

**[02:08:35.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001407)  
Ang Coast Guard, nakasecure sila ng ODA to buy Coast Guard ships.

**[02:08:42.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001408)  
So, last year, we had a discussion.

**[02:08:46.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001409)  
Sabi natin bakit hindi natin gawin the same for the defense, no?

**[02:08:49.320] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001410)  
Whether ODA or other forms of loans.

**[02:08:52.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001411)  
And hence, nalagay natin itong provision.

**[02:08:54.700] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001412)  
But I want to understand, Secretary, how come hindi siya nagamit?

**[02:08:57.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001413)  
Is it too tight?

**[02:08:59.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001414)  
Is it not kulang pa sa language?

**[02:09:03.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001415)  
May we understand kung ano naging result nito, no?

**[02:09:07.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001416)  
It's an innovation, actually, proposed by the Secretary.

**[02:09:11.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001417)  
And sabi ko nga, this is a good way of multiplying the financial capacity of the armed forces or the DND.

**[02:09:20.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001418)  
There are basically two reasons, Your Honor.

**[02:09:25.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001419)  
That there's a tight...

**[02:09:27.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001420)  
fiscal space for what we need.

**[02:09:29.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001421)  
Hence, the provision could not be triggered.

**[02:09:32.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001422)  
And secondly, we were not able, insofar as the proposal sent to us,

**[02:09:39.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001423)  
to get the financing in the rate that we want.

**[02:09:44.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001424)  
It was too expensive, Your Honor, for us to trigger.

**[02:09:48.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001425)  
Hence, we did not trigger, Your Honor.

**[02:09:50.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001426)  
Pero merong sanong opportunity?

**[02:09:53.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001427)  
Meron, pero mataas po ang financing cost.

**[02:09:55.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001428)  
Hindi lang po mataas.

**[02:09:57.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001429)  
Yung cost.

**[02:09:58.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001430)  
Pero pati financing cost, mataas.

**[02:10:01.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001431)  
It's a timing issue na rin.

**[02:10:03.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001432)  
Timing issue, triggering issue, Your Honor.

**[02:10:06.060] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001433)  
And do you propose to include natin ito ulit sa 2027 budget?

**[02:10:13.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001434)  
It will provide us with the flexibility, Your Honor,

**[02:10:16.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001435)  
until the Presidential Decree limiting the amount of foreign borrowings that we can do for defense

**[02:10:25.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001436)  
to 300 million pesos a year.

**[02:10:27.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001437)  
Is that right?

**[02:10:27.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001438)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:10:27.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001439)  
So, we would request it, very humbly request it again, Your Honor.

**[02:10:33.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001440)  
With the assurance that we will not trigger, Your Honor,

**[02:10:36.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001441)  
unless the Department of Finance is comfortable with it

**[02:10:39.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001442)  
and we are comfortable also with the item that is being acquired.

**[02:10:45.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001443)  
With the terms of the financing, no?

**[02:10:48.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001444)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:10:49.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001445)  
Kasi ngayon, admittedly, because of government bonds, everything is expensive right now.

**[02:10:55.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001446)  
Even in India, Your Honor?

**[02:10:57.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001447)  
They increase the yield to 5.13% in government bonds.

**[02:11:01.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001448)  
So, because to defend the Indian currency.

**[02:11:07.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001449)  
So, it's a worldwide phenomenon.

**[02:11:09.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001450)  
Yes, correct.

**[02:11:10.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001451)  
That's actually a reason why peso is weakening because of foreign interest rates.

**[02:11:15.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001452)  
And with critical infrastructure that we need financing to, we understand the mix, Your Honor.

**[02:11:21.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001453)  
But if we find a need that we can fill with the terms that our...

**[02:11:27.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001454)  
We feel, Your Honor, will not be replicated any time,

**[02:11:32.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001455)  
then we will not hesitate to ask the Secretary of Finance to trigger it.

**[02:11:37.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001456)  
How about Secretary ODAs?

**[02:11:39.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001457)  
Kasi ODAs are more competitive.

**[02:11:41.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001458)  
But I'm not very sure whether that's available for defense equipment.

**[02:11:46.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001459)  
I know they're available for PCG, which is a civilian type of operation,

**[02:11:51.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001460)  
law enforcement type of operation.

**[02:11:52.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001461)  
But for defense, I'm not very sure kung available yung mga ganyan sa defense.

**[02:11:56.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001462)  
No, Your Honor.

**[02:11:57.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001463)  
They're not available normally.

**[02:11:59.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001464)  
With Japan, as a matter of fact, there's the official security assistance,

**[02:12:05.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001465)  
which is a grant of 200 million.

**[02:12:07.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001466)  
For the past three years, we were the recipients.

**[02:12:11.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001467)  
But normal ODA financing, which has to conform to OECD and other requirements,

**[02:12:22.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001468)  
still defends.

**[02:12:23.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001469)  
There are some countries that accept it and some none.

**[02:12:27.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001470)  
So the financing for defense are special purpose credit agencies

**[02:12:32.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001471)  
normally tied to exports of the providing country, Your Honor.

**[02:12:39.080] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001472)  
Thank you for that information.

**[02:12:40.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001473)  
We'll propose to the Chairman to re-include the special provision for financing

**[02:12:47.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001474)  
and for foreign and domestic financing.

**[02:12:52.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001475)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[02:12:53.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001476)  
In the same way that we did not trigger this, Your Honor,

**[02:12:56.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001477)  
we did not obligate the full amount of the 50 billion modernization

**[02:13:03.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001478)  
because it will result in a payable of more than the 50 billion this year to about 80.

**[02:13:10.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001479)  
So you're also very conservative in triggering that, Your Honor.

**[02:13:14.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001480)  
Thank you, Secretary.

**[02:13:15.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001481)  
And I'm sure kayo, being a finance guy before,

**[02:13:18.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001482)  
kabisado nyo yung mga ganitong impact to our balance sheet.

**[02:13:24.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001483)  
Yeah.

**[02:13:24.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001484)  
I'll go to...

**[02:13:26.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001485)  
A related item.

**[02:13:28.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001486)  
This is on modernization, Secretary.

**[02:13:32.060] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001487)  
And this has been a really challenging activity of appropriation

**[02:13:42.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001488)  
because malaking amount siya.

**[02:13:45.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001489)  
And last year, there was an allocation in the unprogrammed funds of...

**[02:13:56.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001490)  
50 billion.

**[02:13:58.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001491)  
And na-trigger ba itong unprogrammed funds?

**[02:14:03.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001492)  
I just want to understand whether it has been triggered.

**[02:14:07.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001493)  
Normally yan, nasa dulo na third, fourth quarter.

**[02:14:10.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001494)  
But I'm not...

**[02:14:11.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001495)  
The rationale there is lower down the line items in the unprogrammed

**[02:14:18.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001496)  
para ma-prioritize ka agad yung modernization

**[02:14:21.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001497)  
and ma-trigger at mabigay ka agad sa AFP yung ano.

**[02:14:24.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001498)  
But I want to get an update whether na-trigger

**[02:14:26.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001499)  
ho siya o hindi.

**[02:14:27.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001500)  
Your Honor, in the same manner as we did not trigger the other items,

**[02:14:32.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001501)  
because it would balloon the modernization payables alone for this year,

**[02:14:38.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001502)  
we did not trigger the unprogrammed.

**[02:14:39.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001503)  
And we could not trigger it because the total of the amount,

**[02:14:44.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001504)  
both programmed and unprogrammed,

**[02:14:47.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001505)  
probably would be the full amount of regular modernization infra

**[02:14:52.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001506)  
just to pay the obligations.

**[02:14:56.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001507)  
Okay.

**[02:14:57.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001508)  
So, in other words, it's a...

**[02:14:59.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001509)  
available, but the timing is not favorable?

**[02:15:06.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001510)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:15:07.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001511)  
We obligated 84%.

**[02:15:09.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001512)  
The 12% trigger would cause us to exceed the 50 billion threshold.

**[02:15:16.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001513)  
Actually, our payable for MICA this year would be about 70, right?

**[02:15:22.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001514)  
50.

**[02:15:23.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001515)  
But if we trigger the 12%, it will be about 70.

**[02:15:26.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001516)  
That is the constraint, Your Honor.

**[02:15:28.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001517)  
Okay.

**[02:15:28.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001518)  
That's why we're not triggering the 12%.

**[02:15:31.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001519)  
And because what you said earlier, it's a seller's market.

**[02:15:34.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001520)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:15:35.580] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001521)  
So, mahal ang equipment ngayon?

**[02:15:38.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001522)  
Mahal po ang equipment at pamahal pa ng pamahal

**[02:15:41.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001523)  
because even the raw material inputs sa equipment ay umaakyat po.

**[02:15:47.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001524)  
Critical minerals, semiconductors, electronics, bakal, copper,

**[02:15:55.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001525)  
other inputs, lumalaki.

**[02:15:58.800] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001526)  
So, with what's happening around the world right now,

**[02:16:01.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001527)  
yung mga conflicts,

**[02:16:02.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001528)  
ang impact niyan is yung presyo ng equipment.

**[02:16:06.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001529)  
Tumataas siya at this time.

**[02:16:08.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001530)  
Opo, at saka yung demand po mataas.

**[02:16:10.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001531)  
So, yung presyo po umaakyat.

**[02:16:13.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001532)  
Kasi doon lang po sa munitions,

**[02:16:18.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001533)  
which is not an item of modernization.

**[02:16:20.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001534)  
For 105 and 155 mm ammunition,

**[02:16:27.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001535)  
bumibigyan.

**[02:16:28.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001536)  
Bumibili pa po sa secondary market kung may countries

**[02:16:31.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001537)  
na pwede magbenta ng stock nila

**[02:16:33.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001538)  
para masupply lang doon sa Ukraine or other countries.

**[02:16:40.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001539)  
Or sa Israel, actually.

**[02:16:43.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001540)  
Bumibili nung araw po.

**[02:16:45.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001541)  
So, complicated itong situation natin when it comes to defense equipment.

**[02:16:50.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001542)  
Kasi po...

**[02:16:51.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001543)  
Interrelated itong mga conflicts to us.

**[02:16:54.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001544)  
Historically po, after...

**[02:16:56.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001545)  
After...

**[02:16:58.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001546)  
After the fall of the Berlin Wall,

**[02:17:03.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001547)  
in-scale down po yung tooling of production for defense equipment worldwide.

**[02:17:08.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001548)  
Lalo na po sa Europe.

**[02:17:11.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001549)  
So, ngayon, humahabol pa po sila sa capacity

**[02:17:14.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001550)  
just to supply Ukraine insofar as the EU is concerned.

**[02:17:19.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001551)  
So, wala pa po sila sa full capacity.

**[02:17:22.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001552)  
Actually po, ang...

**[02:17:24.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001553)  
ang Germany po ay...

**[02:17:28.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001554)  
If I'm not mistaken, 500 billion euro ang kanyang modernization.

**[02:17:34.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001555)  
Hindi pa niya na uubos po kasi wala pong capacity to produce pa ang kanyang bansa.

**[02:17:43.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001556)  
So, it is also a question po of availability

**[02:17:47.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001557)  
because of limited lines of production.

**[02:17:52.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001558)  
Because of the scale down of defense equipment.

**[02:17:55.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001559)  
The only country, Your Honor,

**[02:17:58.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001560)  
in our environment,

**[02:18:04.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001561)  
in our like-minded environment,

**[02:18:06.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001562)  
that is equipped but not totally equipped

**[02:18:11.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001563)  
to supply 100% of its clients' demands is Korea.

**[02:18:17.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001564)  
Which is why we have a comprehensive strategic partnership with them

**[02:18:23.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001565)  
and joint development with them.

**[02:18:27.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001566)  
They were the only ones...

**[02:18:28.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001567)  
They were the only ones...

**[02:18:28.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001568)  
They were the only ones, Your Honor,

**[02:18:29.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001569)  
never to slow down their production and industrial base

**[02:18:35.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001570)  
for producing defense equipment.

**[02:18:38.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001571)  
Ngayon kasi, ano talaga eh.

**[02:18:40.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001572)  
I mean, you can see it in the news na

**[02:18:42.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001573)  
parang buong mundo.

**[02:18:48.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001574)  
Something's happening in different parts of the region

**[02:18:50.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001575)  
and definitely a lot of countries now are stocking up,

**[02:18:55.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001576)  
modernizing, increasing their capabilities,

**[02:18:58.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001577)  
and, of course, for us who are also modernizing,

**[02:19:03.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001578)  
it's with very, very limited resources,

**[02:19:08.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001579)  
impacted tayo with those conflicts.

**[02:19:11.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001580)  
Your Honor, may I point out also in ASEAN, for example,

**[02:19:15.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001581)  
Indonesia has ordered 47 new fighters,

**[02:19:21.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001582)  
the top-of-the-line Rafales from France.

**[02:19:27.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001583)  
Malaysia is...

**[02:19:28.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001584)  
It's continuing to expand its capabilities.

**[02:19:33.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001585)  
Actually, they had the missile defense contract

**[02:19:35.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001586)  
which was canceled by, I believe, Finland

**[02:19:38.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001587)  
because they needed to recommit it somewhere else.

**[02:19:42.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001588)  
I'm not sure...

**[02:19:43.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001589)  
No, it's Norway, I think.

**[02:19:46.020] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001590)  
Even Brunei is buying what we heard

**[02:19:50.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001591)  
is that they are acquiring their first F-50s.

**[02:19:54.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001592)  
So everybody within the region is really building up

**[02:19:58.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001593)  
its own...

**[02:19:58.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001594)  
Its own capabilities.

**[02:20:00.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001595)  
Of course, the outlier there is Singapore

**[02:20:03.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001596)  
because it has always done so

**[02:20:05.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001597)  
from day one of its creation, Your Honor.

**[02:20:10.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001598)  
Secretary, is this a...

**[02:20:11.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001599)  
From a policy point of view,

**[02:20:13.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001600)  
is this a signal that the whole...

**[02:20:15.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001601)  
the entire world right now is changing

**[02:20:18.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001602)  
in terms of their mindset on defense?

**[02:20:22.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001603)  
Yes.

**[02:20:22.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001604)  
Because it's becoming more volatile in our region,

**[02:20:27.540] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001605)  
definitely our regions,

**[02:20:28.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001606)  
becoming more volatile,

**[02:20:29.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001607)  
as we can see in the West Philippine Sea.

**[02:20:32.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001608)  
But in the other parts of the world,

**[02:20:34.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001609)  
it's also doing the same.

**[02:20:35.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001610)  
I think, Your Honor,

**[02:20:37.180] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001611)  
it's not only a question of volatility

**[02:20:39.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001612)  
which you will need to deter and to address,

**[02:20:42.580] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001613)  
but a question also of long-term resilience.

**[02:20:47.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001614)  
Many countries do not want to be faced with a situation

**[02:20:50.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001615)  
where they will not be able to deter

**[02:20:53.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001616)  
with the basic capability and weight,

**[02:20:58.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001617)  
or rely totally on an organization

**[02:21:04.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001618)  
or a conglomeration of countries to come to your aid.

**[02:21:09.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001619)  
If at all it comes to that,

**[02:21:12.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001620)  
you will still have to wait for some period of time

**[02:21:16.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001621)  
before help comes.

**[02:21:18.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001622)  
That's why you have to build up that basic amount of resilience

**[02:21:21.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001623)  
to survive in the meantime.

**[02:21:25.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001624)  
But most importantly, Your Honor,

**[02:21:27.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001625)  
it is also...

**[02:21:28.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001626)  
It is also a reality that a more robust defense setup

**[02:21:36.680] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001627)  
deters aggressive actions against...

**[02:21:40.800] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001628)  
And it is a deterrent to aggressive actions and to conflict.

**[02:21:47.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001629)  
More importantly than the need to use it,

**[02:21:51.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001630)  
it is a warning to make an aggressor have a second thought.

**[02:21:58.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001631)  
Before it even attempts to do so.

**[02:22:02.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001632)  
Parang yun yung sinasabi ni Trump,

**[02:22:04.960] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001633)  
peace through strength.

**[02:22:07.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001634)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:22:08.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001635)  
It's just like, you know,

**[02:22:09.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001636)  
building a high fence in your house,

**[02:22:11.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001637)  
it deters people from coming in.

**[02:22:15.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001638)  
It's the same way.

**[02:22:17.440] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001639)  
Thank you, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:22:18.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001640)  
I'll go to Mr. Chairman.

**[02:22:20.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001641)  
Do you have any...

**[02:22:21.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001642)  
Yes, si Senator Tulfo wants to interject

**[02:22:24.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001643)  
with your indulgence, Senate President.

**[02:22:28.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001644)  
Yes, Mr. Secretary,

**[02:22:30.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001645)  
with the indulgence, of course, of our Senate President.

**[02:22:36.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001646)  
Sa arap po ng mga pahayag lately po

**[02:22:38.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001647)  
ng ambasador po ng China

**[02:22:41.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001648)  
at ito patuloy po na tensyon sa West Philippine Sea,

**[02:22:46.000] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001649)  
ano po ang kongkretong paghanda po

**[02:22:48.920] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001650)  
or pwedeng ihanda ng Armed Forces of the Philippines

**[02:22:51.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001651)  
para maprotektahan po ating teritoryo,

**[02:22:54.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001652)  
mga sundalo at mga mamayan

**[02:22:56.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001653)  
sa kabila po ng...

**[02:22:58.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001654)  
mindset

**[02:23:00.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001655)  
na naitatag

**[02:23:02.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001656)  
ng mga dating opisyal

**[02:23:03.540] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001657)  
na isang nuklear lang

**[02:23:05.980] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001658)  
sasabog na tayo,

**[02:23:08.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001659)  
na lulusubin tayo,

**[02:23:09.640] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001660)  
na dudurugin tayo.

**[02:23:11.580] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001661)  
Wala naman po tayong ginagawa, di ba?

**[02:23:13.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001662)  
Na pag-umalma tayo.

**[02:23:15.960] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001663)  
So, yung mga tao naman,

**[02:23:18.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001664)  
kagad sinasabi,

**[02:23:19.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001665)  
magagawa natin,

**[02:23:20.600] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001666)  
bubombahin tayo,

**[02:23:21.720] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001667)  
gegyarahin,

**[02:23:22.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001668)  
because of these pronouncements before

**[02:23:24.960] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001669)  
ng mga officials natin,

**[02:23:27.160] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001670)  
na parang tumatakot,

**[02:23:28.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001671)  
tatak na sa isipan ng mga kababayan natin,

**[02:23:30.360] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001672)  
huwag na lang po tayo magsalita,

**[02:23:31.940] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001673)  
nakakatakot, kawawa tayo.

**[02:23:35.020] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001674)  
Tapos itong recently pa,

**[02:23:37.760] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001675)  
na pailan daw po sila

**[02:23:39.760] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001676)  
ng persona non grata,

**[02:23:42.880] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001677)  
then yung mga opisyal natin,

**[02:23:44.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001678)  
hindi nila tatanggapin doon.

**[02:23:46.720] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001679)  
Yung mga opisyal naman siguro na ito,

**[02:23:48.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001680)  
I'm sure, wala naman silang interest

**[02:23:49.940] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001681)  
din na pumunta ho doon sa lugar na yun.

**[02:23:52.240] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001682)  
Pero, what kind of preparations are you doing now

**[02:23:54.480] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001683)  
to counter this,

**[02:23:56.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001684)  
to allay the fears,

**[02:23:58.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001685)  
of the public,

**[02:23:59.640] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001686)  
na bubombahin tayo,

**[02:24:01.100] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001687)  
nuklear, dodorogin, etc., etc.?

**[02:24:03.940] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001688)  
Mr. Secretary?

**[02:24:05.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001689)  
Unang-una po talagang,

**[02:24:07.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001690)  
we're trying our best really

**[02:24:09.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001691)  
to work not only singly,

**[02:24:13.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001692)  
pero with our other allies

**[02:24:16.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001693)  
to deter any more aggressive physical actions

**[02:24:21.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001694)  
on a military sense by China.

**[02:24:24.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001695)  
And it is not only a threat to us,

**[02:24:28.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001696)  
it is also a threat to Vietnam,

**[02:24:30.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001697)  
to Malaysia,

**[02:24:31.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001698)  
and to Brunei,

**[02:24:35.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001699)  
and a threat to Japan,

**[02:29:50.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001834)  
With his permission, Mr. Chairman,

**[02:29:51.680] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001835)  
there seems to be a glaring discussion

**[02:29:53.760] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001836)  
about the disparity

**[02:29:54.760] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001837)  
on our defense spending

**[02:29:56.440] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001838)  
compared to our regional peers,

**[02:29:58.440] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001839)  
particularly China.

**[02:30:01.120] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001840)  
Kasi yung kanilang defense expenditure is 52 times ng Pilipinas.

**[02:30:07.220] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001841)  
Sa kanila, 335 billion US dollars or roughly 2.1 trillion pesos.

**[02:30:14.560] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001842)  
So how do we compensate? How do we do a David against a Goliath in this regard?

**[02:30:21.040] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001843)  
Kasi wala naman tayo magagawa except mag-improvise, hindi ba?

**[02:30:24.300] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001844)  
So meron ba tayong plano man lang?

**[02:30:27.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001845)  
Sir, the best way is to make ourselves an uninviting target.

**[02:30:32.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001846)  
The hard part there is that an uninviting target would mean only our land domain,

**[02:30:43.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001847)  
which is only 20% of our total territory, 20 to 30%.

**[02:30:51.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001848)  
However, for the land domain to be resilient, we need full expansion.

**[02:30:57.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001849)  
Exploitation and exploration of the 80% maritime domain.

**[02:31:02.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001850)  
So yun ho ang nagpapakomplicate ng situation.

**[02:31:06.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001851)  
Pero I believe ho that with the X billion reported by China,

**[02:31:14.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001852)  
this is even understated because a lot of the budget does not go to the PLA

**[02:31:19.980] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001853)  
but to the maritime militia, the Coast Guard, which are basically under the PLA.

**[02:31:25.880] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001854)

**[02:31:30.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001855)  
So there is no substitute ho but to build a level of resilience

**[02:31:37.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001856)  
which will make it very unattractive for them.

**[02:31:41.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001857)  
There are ways ho and we can also in executive sessions share some thoughts on it, Your Honors.

**[02:31:49.600] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001858)

**[02:31:55.180] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001859)  
Wala tayo.

**[02:31:55.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001860)

**[02:31:57.640] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001861)

**[02:31:59.880] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001862)

**[02:32:01.800] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001863)

**[02:32:07.560] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001864)

**[02:32:15.860] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001866)

**[02:32:27.960] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001869)

**[02:32:39.800] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001872)

**[02:32:46.560] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001873)

**[02:32:48.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001874)

**[02:32:50.240] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-001875)

**[02:33:40.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001885)  
Yun ang balakid po sa atin.

**[02:33:42.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001886)  
Kaya kailangan ho its deterrence rather than retaliation.

**[02:33:48.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001887)  
And that's why ho in our alliances,

**[02:33:50.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001888)  
we are focusing on the non-retaliatory features of...

**[02:33:55.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001889)  
of our defense alliances.

**[02:33:57.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001890)  
But focusing on the deterrence portion po.

**[02:34:04.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001891)  
We will never match China in terms of pound-for-pound capability.

**[02:34:09.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001892)  
But we will make the decision calculus as unfavorable to it as possible.

**[02:34:14.260] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001893)  
That is the goal, Your Honor.

**[02:34:16.200] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001894)  
Thank you.

**[02:34:16.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001895)  
Well, my last question, Mr. Secretary.

**[02:34:18.920] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001896)  
Last year, or just this year po,

**[02:34:21.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001897)  
when I was the Chairman of the Foreign Relations,

**[02:34:23.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001898)  
I was able to talk to

**[02:34:24.920] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001899)  
two or three

**[02:34:25.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001900)  
ambassadors,

**[02:34:26.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001901)  
foreign nations.

**[02:34:26.900] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001902)  
I believe,

**[02:34:27.440] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001903)  
yung isa po,

**[02:34:27.900] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001904)  
nakausap nyo na yung si Ukraine.

**[02:34:30.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001905)  
They are willing to put up a factory here,

**[02:34:33.460] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001906)  
maintenance,

**[02:34:34.400] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001907)

**[02:34:35.520] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001908)  
Matter of fact,

**[02:34:36.080] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001909)  
they're interested po dyan sa may Cavite

**[02:34:38.620] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001910)  
to help us with the drones.

**[02:34:45.600] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001911)  
Have you also spoken to those diplomats?

**[02:34:48.640] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001912)  
Then to other European countries,

**[02:34:51.340] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001913)  
very interested and willing to come here,

**[02:34:54.140] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001914)  
to invest actually,

**[02:34:55.420] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001915)  
provided that we purchase their equipments,

**[02:34:58.500] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001916)  
yung mga drones, sea and air.

**[02:35:01.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001917)  
Marami na po, Your Honor.

**[02:35:03.720] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001918)  
And we are actually, some of those are in the final stages of scoping the engagement.

**[02:35:12.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001919)  
Once again, all of these engagements, Your Honor,

**[02:35:16.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001920)  
will number one place is access securely and location and supply chains, Your Honor.

**[02:35:25.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001921)  
We are pursuing, Your Honor,

**[02:35:28.600] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001922)  
to the best of our ability on a secure basis.

**[02:35:33.320] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001923)  
Tama po.

**[02:35:33.820] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001924)  
Kasi kung titusin, sir,

**[02:35:34.960] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001925)  
hindi po natin kaya bumili ng mga frigates and aircraft carriers, submarines.

**[02:35:40.300] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001926)  
Then may options po tayo ng mga ikang mga drones.

**[02:35:43.880] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001927)  
Sir, one last question.

**[02:35:45.180] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001928)  
I was just wondering,

**[02:35:45.740] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001929)  
may mga donations po tayo ng mga drones sa America, other countries.

**[02:35:49.880] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001930)  
Whatever happened to these drones?

**[02:35:51.380] Senator Erwin T. Tulfo** (asr-001931)  
Nagagamit po ba natin?

**[02:35:53.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001932)  
Limited po ito. Sa

**[02:35:54.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001933)  
lahat po ng

**[02:35:55.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001936)  
dinownload nila, nagagamit.

**[02:35:57.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001937)  
Ang iba pong hindi nagagamit, yung nabili nung araw,

**[02:36:01.860] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001938)  
na hindi po nag-fifit sa ating bagong C4 Command and Control Secure Communication Structures.

**[02:36:14.960] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001939)  
So, mula nung nagumpisa po tayong kumuha ng drones,

**[02:36:19.920] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001940)  
ang environment nun ay purely tactical.

**[02:36:25.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001941)  
So, sa ngayon po, nagiging strategic yung environment.

**[02:36:29.660] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001942)  
Kaya, kailangan po enhanced ang interoperability po nito.

**[02:36:34.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001943)  
So, that is where we are proceeding right now.

**[02:36:38.080] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001944)  
There are uses, ho, tactically for drones and they are being used.

**[02:36:43.760] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001945)  
However, for the bigger picture, ho, we need more multi-purpose drones

**[02:36:48.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001946)  
and we need the facility for quality raw materials

**[02:36:53.100] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001947)  
for this and

**[02:36:55.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001950)  
Actually, precision tooling for it.

**[02:36:58.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001951)  
It's the precision, ho, na kailangan, eh.

**[02:37:00.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001952)  
And, when we learn from the Korean experience,

**[02:37:06.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001953)  
ang kanilang modernization started in 1976

**[02:37:09.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001954)  
when the United States said, hindi na nila susuportahan.

**[02:37:16.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001955)  
But, the defense industry was only an offshoot of basic industry.

**[02:37:25.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001958)  
Your Honor.

**[02:37:26.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001959)  
Because they had basic industry already,

**[02:37:31.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001960)  
which would support the ecosystem for sustainability in the defense industry.

**[02:37:40.780] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001961)  
Steel, electronics, and other capabilities.

**[02:37:45.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001962)  
If we do not also remediate basic industry, Your Honor,

**[02:37:50.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001963)  
for example, the raw materials, secondly, power,

**[02:37:55.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001964)  
we will be constrained to being an assembly location

**[02:38:02.520] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001965)  
also reliant on importation for our outputs.

**[02:38:11.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-001966)  
So, we have to really structure it properly, Your Honor.

**[02:42:46.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002090)

**[02:42:56.420] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002092)  
Because we cannot hope to replicate our bigger country partners also in terms of deployment.

**[02:43:03.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002093)  
For example, one marine expeditionary unit of the United States for an exercise here,

**[02:43:10.060] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002094)  
the pre-deployment is already almost a billion dollars.

**[02:43:13.300] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002095)

**[02:43:16.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002096)

**[02:43:24.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002097)

**[02:43:31.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002098)

**[02:43:35.220] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002099)

**[02:43:40.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002100)  
which has its own air group, its own small country,

**[02:43:46.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-002101)  
so it's also a question right now of scoping the best fit wherever in the world right now.

**[03:24:05.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003763)  
Likewise,

**[03:24:06.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003764)  
Secretary,

**[03:24:07.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003765)

**[03:24:09.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003766)  
I know that you have been very

**[03:24:13.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003768)
