**[03:24:15.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003769)  
Madalas, subject na rin

**[03:24:17.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003770)  
kayo sa attacks.

**[03:24:18.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003771)  
Ang nakakalungkot lang

**[03:24:20.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003772)  
na,

**[03:24:22.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003773)  
May mga lang Pilipino na hindi ko alam bakit ang kalaban naman natin dito,

**[03:24:27.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003774)  
China, dapat lahat tayo nagkakaisa doon pero meron silang mga kampi dito.

**[03:24:32.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003775)  
Like recently, nung nakasaguta ko yung Chinese ambassador,

**[03:24:39.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003776)  
actually pinapractice ko lang yung Mandarin natin,

**[03:24:40.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003777)  
nakalimutan ko na kasi matagal tayong gumraduate galing Saber.

**[03:24:44.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003778)  
Tsaka baka hindi niya naintindihan na yung West Philippine si Ate.

**[03:24:48.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003779)  
Kaya in Chinese, sinabi ko sa kanya na West Philippine si Ate is ours.

**[03:24:52.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003780)  
And nako, in 7 minutes, 500 comments ka agad.

**[03:24:57.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003781)  
So do you think itong mga ito, Secretary, part of ng China ito,

**[03:25:04.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003782)  
na meron silang propaganda machine really to create siguro itong disunity among us?

**[03:25:12.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003783)  
Meron po talaga. Meron po talaga.

**[03:25:15.200] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003784)  
At admitido naman po sila na part of the United Front Works po nila,

**[03:25:20.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003785)  
na pilar ng kanilang...

**[03:25:22.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003786)  
Ito po ay hindi state activity, kung hindi party activity ito.

**[03:25:31.040] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003787)  
Katulad po ng People's Liberation Army,

**[03:25:33.560] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003788)  
hindi parehong organization po yan sa armed forces.

**[03:25:38.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003789)  
Because the PLA is not a sovereign state arm.

**[03:25:43.400] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003790)  
It is a Communist Party of China arm.

**[03:25:47.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003791)  
Separate siya.

**[03:25:49.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003792)  
So talaga po, lahat ng galaw nito,

**[03:25:52.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003793)  
ay motivated by their central committee or standing committee.

**[03:25:57.740] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003794)  
So ang propaganda is the reason also for being of the People's Republic of China.

**[03:26:05.440] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003795)  
It was born through subversion.

**[03:26:09.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003796)  
When, you know, espionage, subversion.

**[03:26:14.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003797)  
And as with a lot of communist governments.

**[03:26:20.160] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003798)  
Although,

**[03:26:22.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003799)  
mga po nagpalit ng anyo.

**[03:26:24.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003800)  
What is, what we observe po dito na dito sa China ang pinakamatinding regime legitimacy support operations ang kanilang foreign policy.

**[03:26:39.120] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003801)  
So talaga pong part and parcel ng kanilang taktika.

**[03:26:44.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003802)  
And makikita naman po natin eh na with a straight face sasabihin that is ours.

**[03:26:49.940] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003803)  
Na wala po silang karapatan.

**[03:26:52.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003804)  
Ngayon, ang pagka ho hindi tayo manindigan laban sa narrative na yan, manonormalize po yan.

**[03:27:00.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003805)  
Manonormalize yan, sasabihin nila, tinanggap nyo na eh, wala kayong i-make.

**[03:27:04.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003806)  
So, mabuti na ho manindigan tayo kaysa sa mawala pa sa atin by default.

**[03:27:10.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003807)  
Secretary, we commend you for standing up doon sa Singapore, no?

**[03:27:14.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003808)  
Nangyayari yun, when you were, sa Seoul ba yun?

**[03:27:17.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003809)  
Singapore.

**[03:27:17.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003810)  
No? Singapore and Seoul.

**[03:27:19.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003811)  
When you were [unclear] with this

**[03:27:21.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003812)  
question, no?

**[03:27:23.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003813)  
Na talagang obviously it was planted.

**[03:27:25.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003814)  
So, ah, we commend you for standing up.

**[03:27:28.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003815)  
Sir Chairman, as an aside, ano?

**[03:27:30.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003816)  
Ah, kaya siguro subject ng, ah, troll attacks si Secretary Guibo.

**[03:27:34.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003817)  
Kasi wala pa nakaka-match doon sa, sa mga fora na naatin na niya.

**[03:27:38.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003818)  
Wala pa nakaka-match doon sa kanyang brilliance.

**[03:27:41.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003819)  
So, so doon lang sila bumabawi.

**[03:27:44.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003820)  
By way of troll attacks, et cetera, et cetera.

**[03:27:47.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003821)  
So, yeah, I second the motion, no?

**[03:27:50.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003822)  
In, ah, commending you for standing up in a manner na talagang hindi na maribat, eh, yung ating position.

**[03:27:58.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003823)  
Thank you.

**[03:27:59.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003824)  
Sir, dahil nung nag-review ako sa bar, isa kayo sa mga advisor ko nung araw, nung panahon na iyon,

**[03:28:06.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003825)  
eto'y part lang ng legal training din natin sa pag-ribat na si Sen. Lacson et al.

**[03:28:14.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003826)  
Ah, kasakasama ko nung nag-review ako nung araw at marami ako natutunan sa kanila.

**[03:28:20.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003827)  
Actually, ah...

**[03:28:22.360] Senator Panfilo "Ping" M. Lacson** (asr-003828)  
Yun ang tinatawag na mutual admiration club, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:28:25.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003829)  
Masundan ko lang ho sa Sen. Lacson, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:28:29.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003830)  
Ah, napanood ko yung dalawang, ah, ah, ah, forum sa, sa Singapore and sa Seoul.

**[03:28:37.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003831)  
And, ah, after that, ah, in Pilipinas, nalagay sa mapa, eh, no?

**[03:28:44.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003832)  
Ah, because that, ah, event was a defense event, ah, attended by many countries, no?

**[03:28:49.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003833)  
So, at, ah, naging very clear and sober as well as rational yung ating position.

**[03:28:56.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003834)  
And we thank the Secretary for delivering that, ah, to the world, no?

**[03:29:00.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003835)  
Kasi hindi lang naman kami yung audience, but ang audience yung buong mundo, eh, no?

**[03:29:05.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003836)  
So, and, ah, explanation was very clear and yet sober.

**[03:29:09.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003837)  
Hindi natin naman, ah, hindi naging bastos, hindi naging walang galang, but very rational.

**[03:29:16.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-003838)  
So, we thank the Secretary for that, ah, message.

**[03:29:19.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003839)  
Thank you, Your Honor, but I must give credit to, to the guidance of the President to us.

**[03:29:24.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003840)  
And secondly, to the collective support of the whole DND and AFP family.

**[03:29:30.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003841)  
Ah, that's where, ah, you know, ah, we have consistent messaging.

**[03:29:35.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003842)  
We, ah, the, ah, agencies under and the department are cohesive and united, ah, in our stand for the West Philippine Sea.

**[03:29:47.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003843)  
So, I mean, the, the expression, ah, in Seoul was instinctive on my part.

**[03:29:53.360] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003844)  
Pero, you cannot avoid also that it is based on my experience, ah.

**[03:30:00.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003845)  
Taking guidance from the President with our experience with the Senate and with each and everybody.

**[03:30:11.460] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003846)  
I'm just the expression of the collective voice of the institution, Your Honor.

**[03:30:17.320] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003847)  
Credit is also due to those individuals and institutions I have mentioned.

**[03:30:24.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003848)  
And we commend you again, Secretary, for that, for standing up for our sovereignty.

**[03:30:30.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003849)  
Nakakalungkot lang na may mga ilan dito.

**[03:30:33.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003850)  
Nakakatawa eh, China yung ano natin dito, ang ating katunggali.

**[03:30:39.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003851)  
Pero sandali lang, ang bilis na may ilang Pilipinas rito, mga troll.

**[03:30:44.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003852)  
Dinidepensa pa yung China, tayo pa yung inaano.

**[03:30:47.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003853)  
Itong mga ito, dapat bordering on ano ito, treso na ba ito siguro?

**[03:30:52.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003854)  
Diba? Sabi ko, mas masahol ba ito sa kalaban, yung pagiging trader sa sariling bayan.

**[03:31:00.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003855)  
So again, we commend you for that, Secretary Teodoro.

**[03:31:05.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003856)  
Yun na lang po, siguro last before we wrap up,

**[03:31:10.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003857)  
we also noticed that may budget na nawala doon sa ating disaster response.

**[03:31:19.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003858)  
Considering that there is a pending El Niño

**[03:31:22.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003859)  
coming next year, itong Super El Niño, may effect ba yung, tanong ko lang,

**[03:31:31.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003860)  
9.4 billion sa Calamity Fund na natanggal po sa inyo.

**[03:31:35.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003861)  
I am just worried that we have to be prepared for this.

**[03:31:42.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003862)  
So ano po maging effect na ito?

**[03:31:43.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003863)  
Hindi ko nga alam kung saan daw nangyari.

**[03:31:45.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003864)  
Whether it was brought to the Department of Agriculture, baka naman diretsyo nila.

**[03:31:50.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003865)  
Pinakita rin kasi kami rin.

**[03:31:52.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003866)  
Direct assistance to farmers and fisher folks.

**[03:31:55.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003867)  
So, your position, Secretary?

**[03:31:58.840] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003868)  
Your Honor, it will not affect because as the Senate President knows,

**[03:32:04.480] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003869)  
as far as last year is concerned,

**[03:32:07.000] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003870)  
I said that the Calamity Fund, it's best downloaded to line agencies where we do the coordination.

**[03:32:15.900] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003871)  
However, for some projects, for example, the integrated logistics centers,

**[03:32:22.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003872)  
for DSWD, DOH, in select areas where, because right now,

**[03:32:31.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003873)  
the warehouses, for example, Your Honor, are in Region 3, for example,

**[03:32:36.640] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003874)  
the two repacking areas of the DSWD, I was informed, were flooded.

**[03:32:43.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003875)  
So, I talked to Senator Gatchalian and offered an area in Fort Magsaysay,

**[03:32:52.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003876)  
for the repacking beside the runway, which is an ideal logistics location.

**[03:33:00.820] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003877)  
So, just to fund the ideal logistics locations, whether it be DSWD or OCD,

**[03:33:10.340] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003878)  
since it will be done also in military bases because of the air transport

**[03:33:20.380] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003879)  
and sea transport.

**[03:33:22.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003880)  
So, that's the only requirement, Your Honor.

**[03:33:27.280] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003881)  
Because now, if we have to augment in the Visayas and Mindanao,

**[03:33:32.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003882)  
but it's good that there is already a supply by Secretary Sherwin,

**[03:33:41.540] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003883)  
Secretary Rex Abinicio, so we just have to fill the gap.

**[03:33:49.500] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003884)  
But normally, before, this used to emanate.

**[03:33:52.240] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003885)  
From Metro Manila yet, which is not logistically ideal.

**[03:33:57.620] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003886)  
But the downloading to other agencies, that's their function, Your Honor,

**[03:34:01.700] DND Secretary Gilberto C. Teodoro Jr.** (asr-003887)  
why we do the coordination.

**[03:34:06.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003888)  
With that, before we end, liwanagin lang natin, hindi natin ina-attack ang Chinese people.

**[03:34:13.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003889)  
Wala tayong bad blood sa kanila.

**[03:34:15.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003890)  
I came from a Chinese school, sa Saber School, pareho kami ng Secretary.

**[03:34:19.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003891)  
We both grew up with our...

**[03:34:22.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003892)  
Our Chinoy friends, batchmates and schoolmates.

**[03:34:25.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003893)  
And we still love our Chinese friends.

**[03:34:28.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003894)  
And recently, I also became an ambassador of a Chinese tire manufacturing company.

**[03:34:32.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003895)  
I use a Chinese vehicle.

**[03:34:34.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003896)  
So, katulad nyo, marami rin akong gamit na galing China.

**[03:34:37.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003897)  
We welcome the people from all backgrounds.

**[03:34:40.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003898)  
And we have valued the friendships and relationships we have built over the years.

**[03:34:43.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003899)  
Pero malinaw po ang ating paninindigan, ang ating values.

**[03:34:46.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003900)  
Ang question natin ay ang aksyon ng Chinese government sa ating West Philippine Sea.

**[03:34:52.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003901)  
I'm sure yan din po ang pinaglalaban ng ating Secretary and the whole DND family.

**[03:34:57.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003902)  
Hindi ang ordinaryong mamamayang Chinese.

**[03:34:59.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003903)  
Yes, we can respect the Chinese people without turning a blind eye to the actions of their government.

**[03:35:05.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003904)  
Yung gobyerno naman nila po ang ating pong...

**[03:35:07.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003905)  
Yung kanilang ginagawa sa ating pong teritoryo, sa ating mangingisda,

**[03:35:13.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003906)  
at syempre sa ating Coast Guard, sa ating Navy.

**[03:35:15.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003907)  
Yan ang ating hindi po tayo pabor.

**[03:35:17.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003908)  
We stand for mutual respect, but we will also stand firm in defending our country's

**[03:35:22.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003909)  
Sovereignty and the dignity of our people.

**[03:35:25.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003910)  
We also reiterate our staunch support for the DND and the Armed Forces

**[03:35:29.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003911)  
in fulfilling its sworn mandate to defend our national interests.

**[03:35:34.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003912)  
With the consent of our Senate President,

**[03:35:36.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003913)  
Win Gatchalian and Senator Ping Lacson,

**[03:35:38.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003914)  
the budget of the Department of National Defense is now deemed,

**[03:35:41.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003915)  
submitted for plenary consideration, subject to discussion

**[03:35:45.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003916)  
and possible budgetary adjustments.

**[03:35:47.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003917)  
The Committee Chair would like to thank everyone,

**[03:35:50.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003918)  
especially our esteemed resource person,

**[03:35:52.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003919)  
for actively participating in today's hearing.

**[03:35:55.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003920)  
With that, this briefing is hereby suspended.

**[03:35:58.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-003921)  
Congratulations.
